cua-driver-rs-v0.46.1cua-driver-rs: v0.46.1 Prerelease2026-11-10T02:02:00Z

Cua Driver 0.46.1

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • the driver inside Cua Spaces leaves updates to the app. (#7153)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-spaces-v0.26.0Cua Spaces v0.26.0 Stable2026-11-09T17:53:04Z

Cua Spaces 0.26.0

Summary

This release adds 2 features and includes 6 fixes.

Features

  • official sign-in on each computer, stop copying sign-ins by default (CUA-3907). (#7045)
  • install-local.sh --development for fast dev-loop builds. (#7019)

Fixes

  • redact tokens in Debug output (#7071). (#7097) reported by @soarkit.

cua-driver-rs-v0.45.4cua-driver-rs: v0.45.4 Prerelease2026-11-09T15:19:15Z

Cua Driver 0.45.4

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • route macOS element-token middle clicks to the target window. (#7074)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v0.22.6 Stable2026-11-10T13:48:14Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.6

Summary

This patch release ships three fixes: team lock recovery preservation, cleanup logic that protects active temporary directories, and question terminal state preservation for reliable answer handling.

Fixed

  • PRESERVE SHARED LOCKS DURING RECOVERY (#4075): Team membership, scaling, task-claim, mailbox, and shared dispatch locks now preserve stale locks and report team_lock_recovery_required instead of deleting another operation's replacement lock. After a crash, confirm all users of the lock have stopped before manually removing the reported directory; automatic crash recovery is intentionally disabled.
  • PRESERVE ACTIVE TEMPORARY DIRECTORIES DURING CLEANUP (#4076): omx cleanup now preserves temporary directories and only reaps orphaned OMX MCP processes. A directory name and modification time do not establish ownership, and matching temporary directories may contain active projects or uncommitted work.
  • PRESERVE THE FIRST TERMINAL QUESTION STATE (#4077): A stale question UI, renderer replacement, or timeout handler could overwrite an accepted answer or report it as a failure. Question state transitions are now serialized and the first terminal state is preserved across API, UI, error, and supersede paths. Only the winning answer runs answer side effects; terminal records survive late submissions and cancellation.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.6.

Verification

  • Full CI on dev candidate (candidate details recorded in release readiness).
  • PR #4075 CI passed on its merged head.
  • PR #4076 CI passed on its merged head.
  • PR #4077 CI passed on its merged head.

Contributors

Thanks to @dependabot[bot], @shenyankm, Watch, and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

v0.22.5 Stable2026-11-09T19:32:44Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.5

Summary

This patch release ships two critical team runtime safety improvements: fail-closed task-read error handling and memory/secrets/API preservation across state mutations.

Fixed

  • FAIL CLOSED ON TASK READS AND STALE REPLY TARGETS (#4067): Team runtime now fails closed on task read failures, handling corrupted task files and unresolvable reply targets instead of silently ignoring them. This prevents task loss and state inconsistency during malformed or id-mismatched operations.
  • PRESERVE MEMORY WRITES, SECRETS, AND API AVAILABILITY (#4068): Integration and worker operations now preserve memory writes, API availability state, and secret values across state mutations. Previous mutations could lose in-flight writes, reset API state during recovery, or drop configured secrets, causing workflows to restart or fail. This fix ensures state consistency across all operations.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.5.

Verification

  • Full CI on dev candidate (candidate details recorded in release readiness).
  • PR #4067 CI passed on its merged head.
  • PR #4068 CI passed on its merged head.

Contributors

Thanks to @shenyankm and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 6 releases · 4 stable · 2 prerelease
v0.9.30 Stable2026-11-10T12:44:28Z

HyperFrames v0.9.30

Released on 2026-11-10.

Studio stays responsive on large projects while it keeps history. The preview server now refuses writes from other sites and host names it does not serve, so a tunnel or remote setup may need one setting.

Fixes

  • CLI: When hyperframes reverse needs you to sign in, the agent now tells you why in one message with the link (and the device code on a remote terminal). The reverse skill also spots an outdated CLI install and runs the latest release instead (007664e25, #7488).
  • AWS Lambda: The Lambda handler ZIP installs its runtime from a committed lockfile, with install scripts turned off (f9255e39a, #7485).
  • Studio Server: On large projects, Studio's history checks no longer hold up the server (61e720b0e, #7491).
  • Gcp Cloud Run: The Cloud Run image pins its base image and checks its browser and bun downloads by checksum (c6d243a2d, #7487).
  • Studio Server: The preview server refuses writes from other sites and requests for host names it does not serve. Local use needs no change. If you open hyperframes preview or the Studio dev server through a tunnel, Codespaces or a remote dev host, that setup can now be refused with a 403: name the host in HYPERFRAMES_PREVIEW_ALLOWED_HOSTS (comma separated), for example HYPERFRAMES_PREVIEW_ALLOWED_HOSTS=demo.tunnels.example hyperframes preview (f318d3d08, #7490).

earendil-works/pi ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v1.5.2 Stable2026-11-10T12:25:43Z

New Features

  • Fork sessions whose folder is gone: when a session's original folder was moved or deleted, you can now fork it into the current folder instead of only continuing, so later resumes no longer ask. See Continue or switch sessions.

Changed

  • When a session's working directory no longer exists, for example after the project folder was renamed, interactive mode now offers Continue, Fork, or Cancel instead of only continuing. Continue runs the session in the current directory without storing the choice, as before; Fork copies it into the current directory as a new session with a new ID, like --fork, so the next resume no longer asks. This applies at startup (--session, --resume), to /resume, to /import, and to ctx.switchSession() from extensions (#11975 by @petrroll, fixes #11060)

Fixed

  • Fixed /import of a session whose working directory no longer exists leaving a copy in the session directory, and continuing it leaving a second copy with the same ID (s.jsonl and s-1.jsonl); the failed copy is now removed (#11975 by @petrroll, fixes #11060)
  • Fixed Anthropic OAuth requests reporting an outdated Claude Code version

View release →

v1.5.1 Stable2026-11-09T23:47:44Z

New Features

  • Shell login and logout: use pi auth login <provider> and pi auth logout <provider> without starting a session, with API-key and no-browser sign-in options. See Credential commands.
  • Idle prompt cache warming: eligible caches now stay warm for up to 30 minutes between runs when a refresh is expected to pay for itself. See cacheWarming.
  • Anthropic message threads: requests send only new messages while the server continues the conversation, with full-request fallback when earlier context changes. See Anthropic compatibility.
  • Richer Mermaid diagrams: pie charts, author-defined styles, clickable labels on supported terminals, and diagrams in expanded branch and compaction summaries. See Display settings and Markdown transformers.
  • GitHub Copilot classifiers: call Microsoft-Decision-1 through codemode models.classify() for typed decisions and probabilities. See Use classifier models.
  • Package update summaries: pi update --extensions reports each package's npm version or git commit change and distinguishes unchanged, skipped, and warned updates. See Update Pi or packages.

Breaking Changes

  • Custom GrepOperations.readFile callbacks are no longer called for context lines. The field remains accepted as optional and deprecated so existing integrations still compile. The grep tool now takes context lines from ripgrep and no longer reads matched files itself. To search a remote system, override grep's execute(); see Remote Execution (#9276)
  • OpenAI-compatible models no longer send "tools": [] when all tools are disabled in a session that has used tools; the field is omitted. This fixes 400 errors (tools must not be an empty array) from vLLM 0.20+, also behind LiteLLM. If your proxy requires tools once the history has tool calls, such as older LiteLLM versions in front of Anthropic, set compat.requiresToolsWithToolHistory: true for that model in models.json (#11843)

Added

  • pi update now lists each updated package with its npm version or git commit change, for example npm:foo 1.2.0 -> 1.3.0, and prints Packages are already up to date when nothing changed. When nothing changed but an update printed a warning, for example because npm left the old version installed after resolving stale registry metadata, it prints No packages were updated, see the warnings above instead; in offline mode it prints Skipped package updates (offline mode). PackageManager.update() reports each changed package as a new result progress event whose message holds that line (#11707, #11711)
  • Anthropic sessions now use message threads: each request sends only the new messages and the server continues from the previous reply, falling back to the full request when the conversation changed (for example after compaction or a tool list change). On by default for the anthropic provider on api.anthropic.com; set compat.supportsMessageThreads in models.json to enable it for gateways that forward the thread field, or to turn it off. Cache warming does not touch the session's thread (#11545)
  • pi.registerMarkdownTransformer() takes an optional { messageTypes } that selects the Markdown surfaces a transformer runs on. Besides user, assistant, and thinking Markdown, transformers can now opt into custom (custom messages and skill blocks), summary (branch and compaction summaries), and changelog (What's New, /changelog, the Extension Updates notice, and the Update Available note). Without options a transformer runs on user, assistant, and thinking Markdown as before. The built-in Mermaid renderer now also draws diagrams in expanded branch and compaction summaries
  • pi auth login <provider> logs in to a provider from the shell without starting a session, for example pi auth login radius. It signs in with an account in the browser and exits; --api-key saves an API key instead, and --no-browser uses device code or copy-code sign-in instead. Logging in to Radius also configures the Radius MCP server. pi auth logout <provider> removes the stored credentials, like /logout
  • ctx.ui.custom() overlays now honor the blocked option: pi reports blocked program status (OSC 7501) from when the overlay is shown until it calls done(), also while it is hidden with setHidden(). Closing one overlay keeps other open questions blocked (#11816)
  • Added the qwen-token-plan-individual-cn provider for Qwen Token Plan Individual on the China endpoint, using QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_CN_API_KEY (#7989 by @bigoldcat123)
  • Added Microsoft-Decision-1 classifier support through GitHub Copilot, including codemode models.classify() (#11679 by @Marvae)
  • Added compat.promptCacheKeyMode ("auto", "enabled", or "disabled") to enable prompt cache keys for compatible proxies or omit them for endpoints that reject them. Set it in models.json when using pi (#9442 by @dannote)
  • Added compat.requiresNonNullAssistantContent for OpenAI-compatible endpoints that require an empty string instead of null in assistant messages containing only tool calls. Set it in models.json when using pi (#8118 by @gaoyk19)
  • Added syntax highlighting for Dart, Nix, Groovy, and Gradle files in read and write views (#11643)
  • Added GLM 5.3 to the Qwen Token Plan Individual model allowlist (#11658)
  • Added Claude Fable 5.2 (claude-fable-5-2) to the anthropic provider ($8/$40 per million input/output tokens, 1M context, 128K output). Like Fable 5.1 it uses per-message effort and mid-conversation system messages; requests its safety classifiers refuse before any output are answered by Claude Opus 5 through server-side fallback and billed at Opus 5's price. Claude Fable 5.2 and Mythos 5.2 on other providers, such as OpenRouter's anthropic/claude-fable-5.2, get the same per-message effort support

Changed

  • The default model for the anthropic provider is now Claude Opus 5.5 (claude-opus-5-5) instead of Claude Opus 4.8
  • Default models moved to newer models at the same or a lower price: amazon-bedrock Claude Opus 5.5, azure GPT-6.1 Sol, google and google-vertex Gemini 4 Argon, vercel-ai-gateway GLM 5.3, baseten GLM 5.3, minimax and minimax-cn MiniMax-M3, qwen-token-plan and qwen-token-plan-cn Qwen3.8 Max, opencode-go Kimi K3.1 Pro, and Kimi K2.7 Code instead of Kimi K2.6 for moonshotai, moonshotai-cn, huggingface, opencode, openrouter, cloudflare-workers-ai and cloudflare-ai-gateway
  • cacheWarming now defaults to "idle" instead of "streaming": pi also keeps eligible prompt caches warm for up to 30 minutes between agent runs, but only when a refresh is expected to pay for itself. Set "cacheWarming": "streaming" in global settings for the previous behavior
  • Consecutive cache warms now show as one Cache warmed: $<total> line with the cumulative cost instead of one line per warm
  • The OpenAI Codex provider is shown as "OpenAI Codex" again instead of "OpenAI Codex (legacy)" in /login and the model selector
  • The /login and /logout provider lists and /login <provider> completions now show the preferred partners Radius and OpenRouter first, then all other providers alphabetically. In the provider lists, partners are marked with ✦, the selected Radius row shimmers in the Radius logo colors and the selected OpenRouter row glows in OpenRouter's brand color (lime on dark themes, violet on light themes)
  • Upgraded terminal Mermaid rendering to support more diagram types, including pie charts, author-defined styles, and clickable labels on terminals with hyperlink support (#8158 by @xl0)

Fixed

  • Fixed the grep tool's glob matching from the folder pi was started in instead of from path: with path: "repo", glob: "migrations/main/*.js" returned No matches found even though repo/migrations/main/a.js exists. Globs are now relative to path, the same form grep prints its results in, also when path is a symlink. A leading ./ (./src/*.ts) is ignored instead of matching nothing (#11807, #10998)
  • Fixed prompt template arguments dropping apostrophes and merging words: an apostrophe inside a word, as in doesn't, no longer opens a quote; a quote with no closing match is kept as text; and \" inside "..." (or \' inside '...') gives a literal quote (#11861)
  • Fixed the bash tool reporting only Command timed out when the command had already exited but a background process it started kept writing to its output until the timeout. The error now also reports the shell's exit code and when it exited (#11613)
  • Images in steering and follow-up messages, including prompt() while streaming, are now resized like images in idle prompts instead of being sent and saved at full size (#11738)
  • A failed token refresh of an MCP connection no longer registers an OAuth client or replaces the PKCE verifier of a sign-in in progress, which made that sign-in fail. MCP sign-in now refreshes the stored tokens with the stored client even when the loopback callback port changed, instead of registering a new client and opening the browser. Ctrl+C in /mcp and the --timeout of pi mcp login now also stop a sign-in while it waits up to 25 seconds for another pi process's token refresh, and a refresh lock that fails for another reason than that wait now fails the sign-in instead of opening the browser.
  • Fixed pi mcp list reporting MCP servers with "auth": { "provider": "<provider>" }, such as Radius, as needing sign-in although the provider was signed in: the shell command sent no token. It now sends the provider's /login token and refreshes it when it expired. For these servers the sign-in hint names /login <provider> instead of pi mcp login, and pi mcp login and pi mcp logout point to /login and /logout instead of failing with "does not use OAuth"
  • Fixed RPC mode ignoring an extension's ctx.shutdown() while idle, for example from a timer: the process kept running until the client sent another command. pi now shuts down right away when idle, and a shutdown requested by an extension command waits until the command's prompt response is written (#11599)
  • Fixed changelog notes repeating on every fresh launch when the installed package version has a prerelease suffix, such as 0.1.5-20261020.7: the saved version was read as 0.1.0, so the 0.1.1 to 0.1.5 notes counted as new each time. A saved version without major.minor.patch now shows no notes and is replaced by the installed version, instead of showing the whole changelog (#11703)
  • Fixed RPC mode cutting off shutdown cleanup, such as a pending MCP token refresh, when stdin closed and SIGTERM arrived together, which process managers like Paseo and systemd do. Pi lost the refreshed token and the MCP server revoked the sign-in. Once a shutdown has started, a stdin EOF or a single signal no longer interrupts it; a second signal still exits at once. Print mode likewise no longer dies from a signal that arrives during its final cleanup (#11599)
  • Fixed the grep tool reading every matched file into memory when context was set, which ran out of memory on large files; context now comes from ripgrep, which also fixes UTF-16 files and lone CRs in matched lines, and the match limit notice no longer appears when exactly limit matches exist (#9276, #11423)
  • Fixed the find tool reporting the result limit when exactly limit files match, and limit: 0 in find and ls returning every result or an empty listing; limits below 1 now mean 1 (#7121)
  • Fixed grep match lines, codemode output, compaction summaries of tool results, and MCP error and server summaries cutting an emoji or other non-BMP character in half when truncating, which left an invalid lone surrogate in the text (#7121)
  • Fixed the find tool on Windows returning no results for patterns with Windows separators such as src\**\*.ts, and for every path pattern such as src/**/*.ts with fd 10.5.1, which pi now downloads. Windows path patterns now match the same way with every fd version: src/**/*.ts also finds files directly in src/, and src/*.ts no longer finds files in subdirectories (#9262)
  • Fixed the read tool loading whole files into memory, which ran out of memory on large files even with offset/limit; it now streams the file and keeps only the shown lines. Custom ReadOperations can stream too by providing the new optional createReadStream (#9654)
  • Fixed OAuth logins such as openai and anthropic failing with invalid_grant after pi was stopped while it refreshed an expired token, for example an RPC client that stops pi right after startup while the background catalog refresh runs. The token endpoint had already replaced the refresh token, but pi exited before saving the new one. On exit, pi now waits for a token refresh in progress to be saved (#11692)
  • Fixed Ctrl+P not switching to the only model in scope (or the only available model) when the active model is a different one; it showed "Only one model in scope" instead. Cycling forward from a model outside the scope now also selects the first scoped model instead of skipping it (#11806)
  • Fixed /reload and session switches leaving extension overlays on screen when an extension had more than one ctx.ui.custom({ overlay: true }) open: only the topmost overlay was removed, and the others kept focus and input although the extension that opened them was gone. All extension overlays, including hidden ones, are now removed and disposed; pi's own overlays stay open
  • Fixed /mcp sign-in staying on "Connecting…" for minutes after a successful browser sign-in, with Ctrl+C doing nothing: pi waited for the browser to drop an unused connection to the OAuth callback port. The callback server now closes remaining connections when it shuts down (#11311)
  • Fixed pi aborting (zsh: abort) after repeated image reads, reported on macOS arm64 with Node 24.16. Each image resize starts a worker thread, and pi did not wait for it to stop, so the next resize started a new worker while the previous one was still shutting down. The crash is most likely a Node bug that this overlap triggers and could not be reproduced on Linux; image resizes now wait for the previous worker to stop, which adds about 15 ms per image (#10695)
  • Fixed tool_search and searchTools() not finding a tool by property names and descriptions behind a local $ref, as in MCP input schemas that put their models in $defs. Referenced definitions are read once, so recursive schemas do not loop, and definitions nothing references stay out of the search text (#11803)
  • Fixed the codemode resolves to summary of a tool leaving out field names behind a local $ref, for example structuredContent? instead of structuredContent?: { results } for MCP output schemas that put their models in $defs (#11803)
  • Fixed every codemode script failing with URI malformed when any callable tool, such as an MCP tool, had a $ref with a malformed percent escape (#/$defs/%E0) in its schema, even scripts that do not call that tool. The parameter now renders as unknown in the tool's declaration (#11803)
  • Fixed ctrl+o not expanding or collapsing the output of ! and !! commands run while the agent is working. New !/!! output now starts in the current expand state, the output no longer disappears when a steering or follow-up message is queued, and it moves into the chat when the agent finishes instead of on the next submitted message (#11875)
  • Fixed resuming a session restoring an old thinking level after /tree navigated to before a /thinking change. Like the model, the live thinking level is now recorded on the new branch with the next prompt (#11451)
  • Fixed inline images in fullscreen mode showing only their first row in Orca, while regular mode showed them in full. Fullscreen now draws Kitty images after the rows they cover on every terminal, as it already did for WezTerm (#11835)
  • Fixed the edit tool rewriting the line endings of untouched lines in files with mixed CRLF/LF or lone CR line endings, and running out of memory on large files after the file was already written; the diff now covers only the changed lines and their surroundings and is computed before writing (#8544)
  • Fixed the Windows installer (irm https://pi.dev/install.ps1 | iex) aborting with NativeCommandError in Windows PowerShell 5.1 when npm or Node.js printed any warning, for example npm warn Unknown user config from an unknown .npmrc key. It failed before the action menu when migrating an npm installation, and during managed installs. An invalid managed-install.json also aborted the installer instead of being reported (#11944)
  • Fixed pi update --models failing with Could not refresh model catalogs: openai: OAuth refresh failed for openai: ... invalid_grant when a provider's login had gone stale. Built-in providers whose catalog comes from pi.dev, such as openai, no longer refresh their OAuth token for a catalog refresh at all. For providers that list models with the login, such as Radius and OpenRouter, a failed token refresh now prints a warning naming the provider and pi auth login <provider>, and the other catalogs still refresh; the command fails only for other errors, such as a network failure
  • Fixed the powershell tool reporting Command exited with code 1 under Windows PowerShell 5.1 when a native command succeeded but its stderr was redirected, as in git push 2>&1 | Select-Object -Last 3. Windows PowerShell 5.1 turns that stderr into error records; such commands now report the exit code PowerShell 7 reports (#8842)
  • Fixed deleting a paste marker with Backspace corrupting later short markers such as [paste #2], causing the marker text to be submitted instead of its pasted content (#11923)
  • Fixed dropped provider streams reported as "cut short after the reply had begun" not triggering automatic retries (#11879)
  • Fixed the subagent extension example passing Bun's Windows virtual entry path as part of the prompt when spawning a compiled pi binary (#11865)
  • Fixed the model selector and llama.cpp Hugging Face search moving the selection when a background catalog refresh or search result arrives (#9109)
  • Fixed internal cursor markers leaking into terminal output, including with multiple markers or horizontally clipped content (#9441 by @muyiyr)
  • Fixed the subagent extension example showing running tasks as completed, losing subprocess failure diagnostics, and failing to stop child processes on abort. Agent discovery and trust checks now use each task's working directory, and single and chain results are truncated without losing the full tool details (#8250 by @terrorobe)
  • Fixed large pasted text being replaced by literal paste markers when switching editors, opening custom UI, or restoring queued messages (#10146 by @w-winter)
  • Fixed prompts and session-tree navigation overlapping during asynchronous input hooks, authentication, or branch summaries, which could install the wrong conversation context (#9155 by @acmerfight)
  • Fixed Claude Sonnet 5 requests sending an unsupported temperature parameter (#7961 by @jingtao-wisdomgraph)
  • Fixed extensions loaded through symlinked paths failing to resolve their dependencies by resolving the entry file's real path before importing it (#8112 by @cad0p)
  • Fixed the plan-mode extension example missing completion markers in thinking blocks and common completion phrases, dropping formatted or long plan steps, and sending an extra model turn to acknowledge completion (#7950 by @giannisCKS)
  • Fixed OpenAI Responses requests sending session_id instead of the documented session-id affinity header (#11639)
  • Fixed delivered image-only steering and follow-up messages remaining in the pending queue (#8612 by @wutongyuonce)
  • Fixed cancellation during lazy provider loading or authentication being reported as an error instead of an abort (#8635 by @Mallikarjun-0)
  • Fixed session runtime disposal tearing down extensions before active tools finished aborting and their results were recorded (#9126 by @acmerfight)
  • Fixed the session-tree search cursor not being shown or positioned for input methods (#9157 by @Perlence)
  • Fixed SDK and RPC reloads replacing extensions while a response or compaction was still running; reload now asks the caller to wait (#9222 by @acmerfight)
  • Fixed rendered edit diffs losing shared indentation when a change inserted text at the start of a line (#9274 by @dannote)
  • Fixed ! and !! commands omitting the truncation notice and full-output file link when earlier output chunks had been discarded (#10165 by @acmerfight)
  • Fixed extension selection dialogs growing beyond the terminal height; they now show a scrolling window of up to ten options (#11072 by @DivineDominion)
  • Fixed pi config ignoring custom keybindings from keybindings.json (#11651)
  • Fixed concurrent OAuth refreshes through symlinked paths to the same auth.json by sharing the lock on the resolved file (#11655)
  • Fixed provider connection errors hiding their underlying cause, such as connection refusal or certificate errors, behind generic messages (#11695)
  • Fixed Ctrl+V not pasting clipboard images or text on Windows and WSL; both Ctrl+V and Alt+V are now default paste shortcuts there (#11765)
  • Fixed llama.cpp connection errors omitting the server URL and underlying network error (#11745)
  • Fixed Alacritty hyperlinks not being enabled when TERM_PROGRAM is unset; detection now also uses TERM and ALACRITTY_WINDOW_ID (#11767)
  • Fixed OpenAI Codex SSE retries retaining abort listeners after each backoff, causing listener buildup on repeated retries (#11708)
  • Azure OpenAI models now keep OpenAI's long-context price tier, so requests above 272K input tokens are costed at Azure's long-context rates (#11948)
  • Fixed Anthropic and Bedrock sending strict: "prefer" tools strict again on every request after Claude rejected them for exceeding its strict tool limits, which cost a rejected request (400) per turn. Later requests with the same sessionId now send those tools non-strict right away, until cleanupSessionResources(sessionId) (#11626)
  • Fixed tool arguments for Type.BigInt() and Type.Undefined() parameters being rejected: they convert as TypeBox converted them ("42" and 42 to 42n, null to undefined), now that pi-schema supports both types. Type.Void() still rejects every JSON argument.
  • Fixed Azure GPT-6 Astra, Luna, Sol, GPT-6.1 Sol, and GPT-6.1 Terra using a 272K context window instead of the 1,050,000 tokens Azure Foundry deploys them with (#11742)
  • Fixed OpenRouter rejecting requests with a 400 (This endpoint's maximum context length is ...) for models whose output limit is close to the context window, such as DeepSeek flash. OpenRouter counts the input with its own tokenizer, which can be several percent higher than the provider's count, and pi left only 4,096 tokens spare. pi then treated the 400 as a context overflow and compacted the session, even at around 20% of the window. The default output limit now leaves 15% of the estimated input unused, and near a full window at most 32,768 output tokens are requested (#10497)
  • Fixed Anthropic non-strict tool schemas dropping root $defs and definitions, which left property $refs pointing at nothing. Claude then produced wrong arguments for such tools (common in MCP servers), for example a JSON string instead of an object, and some Anthropic-compatible gateways rejected the request outright (#9557)
  • Fixed OpenCode Go Claude Haiku 5.5 failing with a 400 (Invalid signature in thinking block ... bound to a different conversation) after the tool list, system prompt, or earlier history changed, which left the session failing on every retry. OpenCode Go and Zen Claude 5.5 models, Opus 5, and Fable 5.1 now use per-message effort and send thinking.block_binding: drop_block, like the Anthropic provider (#10954)
  • Fixed Markdown displaying HTML comments (<!-- ... -->) as visible text (#11710)
  • Fixed codemode console.* output and thrown non-Error values printing NaN, Infinity, and -Infinity as null (#11901)
  • Fixed large MCP stdio messages being repeatedly copied and scanned as chunks arrived, causing slow tool responses (#11761)
  • Fixed MCP Streamable HTTP SSE replies with CR-only line endings failing with "stream ended without a response" (#11764)
  • Fixed oversized MCP SSE responses keeping their HTTP connections open after the message-size limit rejected them (#11729)
  • Fixed MCP stdio server subprocesses surviving disconnect or pi exit on POSIX when the server's main process had already exited (#11721)
  • Fixed MCP Streamable HTTP JSON replies bypassing message-size limits and HTTP errors buffering entire response bodies. Oversized replies and error-body content beyond the first 8 KiB are now cancelled (#11335)
  • Fixed valid tool arguments being rejected when their schema combined local $refs with a relative root $id, such as schemas/tool.json (#11697)
  • Fixed the find tool starting an untracked fd process when cancelled during its parent-directory lookup (#11974 by @petrroll)

opendatalab/MinerU ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
mineru-4.2.0-releasedMinerU 4.2.0 Stable2026-11-10T11:45:47Z

4.2.0

MinerU 4.2.0 重点升级 WebUI 交互与结果溯源，改善复杂 PDF、公式和表格的解析质量，并完善设备选择、模型缓存、服务管理与输入安全。以下为相对 4.1.0 的累计更新，包含 4.1.x 期间已发布的改进。

WebUI 交互与结果溯源

  • 新增原文与结果双向定位：点击 PDF 或图片中的区域，即可定位并高亮对应的解析内容；点击结果中的段落、标题、公式或图表，也可返回原文位置。跨页合并内容支持切换来源页。
  • 增强布局可视化：合并后的段落、列表和表格可关联多个原文区域，并通过连线展示合并关系，方便检查阅读顺序、内容归属和跨页合并结果。
  • 新增结果目录导航：根据标题生成可折叠目录，支持快速跳转到对应内容。
  • 重新设计上传区域：集中呈现文件选择、拖放、粘贴、文件类型、上传进度和取消操作，让上传过程更清晰。
  • 重做档位与页范围控件：采用更紧凑的布局，优化选择与拖动体验，并展示解析服务的 CPU/GPU 运行状态。
  • 加快 PDF 预览：选择本地 PDF 后，浏览器直接预览完整文档，并行完成上传，减少预览等待和重复传输。
  • 保持解析前后的预览状态：解析完成后复用已加载的查看器，保留缩放状态，并以叠加标框展示布局结果。
  • 减少重复处理：复用已准备的输入文件、语义结果和素材，减少原文 PDF、布局 PDF 及预览文件的重复生成，加快结果预览与导出。

PDF、公式与表格解析

  • 改进行内公式识别：Standard/Advanced 在 PDF 原生文本路径中，使用 VLM 识别表外行内公式区域；识别结果为空或无效时，保留局部公式识别模型回退。
  • 保护脚注公式并改进页边区域处理：修复脚注公式被错误改标或过滤的问题，减少公式、编号与正文之间的归属错误。
  • 改进嵌套与重叠区域处理：优化重复区域筛选与合并范围，减少重复文本和过度合并；修正图内标注的归属，并改善合并图片的阅读顺序。
  • 统一布局检测行为：恢复 ONNX 与 Torch 后端的逐类输出阈值，提高不同后端处理结果的一致性。
  • 改善原生文本恢复：完善空 ToUnicode 映射下的字符恢复，并在后置 OCR 和失败恢复后清理残留的 Unicode 私用区字符，减少缺字和乱码。
  • 保留仿斜体正文：修复整行字符经过倾斜变换时，正常正文被误判并丢失的问题。
  • 修复旋转页面中的表格方向判断：结合页面旋转信息解释原生文字方向，减少表格被错误旋转的问题。
  • 保持表格内图片与公式完整：避免表格恢复过程中将图片、公式拆散，或错误分配到多个单元格。
  • 改善跨页表格输出：保留单元格拼接处的文本边界，完善空白续接、稀疏空白行和共享跨行单元格的处理。

原生文档解析

  • 利用 Tagged PDF 结构语义：Flash 原生 PDF 文本解析可利用 Tagged PDF/PDF/UA 文档中的结构信息与阅读顺序；未覆盖或不可信的内容继续由几何规则处理。
  • 改进段落续接：结合缩进、句末位置等页面证据，改善大写字母开头的续接判断，以及跨页、跨栏段落合并。
  • 改进原生表格恢复：修复异常字形边界导致的行划分错误，以及部分数字表格中跨行单元格的归属问题。
  • 改善字体粗细判断：增强嵌入字体的字重解析，减少正常正文被误判为粗体的情况。
  • 改善 Office 图片异常处理：对部分超出图片处理资源限制的内容使用占位表示，避免单张图片导致整份文档解析失败。

设备选择、档位推荐与硬件诊断

  • 统一进程内的设备选择：模型加载、自动档位推荐和 llama.cpp 自动模式共享已确定的运行设备，减少重复探测与不同入口之间的行为差异。
  • 改善多显卡识别：通过物理 PCI 地址关联 Windows GPU 与显存信息，提高多显卡及同型号设备的匹配准确性。
  • 调整自动档位推荐：根据小模型后端实际选择的设备推荐档位。GPU 可开放全部档位；CPU 可用逻辑核数达到 4 核时开放 Basic，达到 12 核时开放 Standard。
  • 完善 ONNX 独显自动选型：Windows/Linux 自动模式增加独显总显存检查。总显存按 GiB 换算并四舍五入后达到 6 及以上，才直接选择 GPU；容量不足或无法确认时使用 CPU。
  • 增加设备状态查询：解析服务健康接口可返回已初始化的 CPU/GPU 运行状态，查询本身不触发设备选择。
  • 加快硬件诊断：并行执行隔离的运行时探测，按节输出报告；统一容量显示与设备摘要，并在 JSON 中保留完整诊断信息及候选设备的选择原因。
  • 加快 CLI 帮助与版本查询：按需加载命令入口，避免仅查看帮助或版本时初始化运行环境、读取用户配置。
  • 改善运行时退出：完善 VLM 关闭与资源释放，减少进程退出阶段的清理失败。

Router 与 API

  • 新增动态上游节点管理：支持在运行期间查询、注册和移除远程解析节点，无需重启 Router。
  • 支持节点平滑移除：先停止向节点分配新任务，待已有任务、传输和结果引用释放后，再完成移除。
  • 支持 Router 级联：复用现有 V1 协议与结果租约，支持将下层 Router 作为上游节点接入，便于组织多层服务入口。
  • 提供独立管理认证：动态节点管理使用独立的管理凭证，与解析请求认证分离。
  • 修复路径识别并统一文件名校验：修复 CLI 相对路径和隐藏文件路径的格式识别；统一 Tasks、Router 与同步上传入口的文件名校验，拒绝路径型名称和控制字符。

模型下载与缓存校验

  • 引入模型文件清单：为整仓、目录和单文件资源记录预期文件及大小，在缓存复用时检查文件缺失、截断、失效链接和清单异常。
  • 支持离线校验：正常缓存可在不联网的情况下完成校验与复用，无需读取大体积权重文件计算哈希。
  • 改善增量修复：识别缺失或大小不符的文件，在重新下载时修复，并继续复用有效文件。
  • 兼容旧完成标记：旧缓存通过必要文件与分片检查后，可继续离线使用；具备网络条件时，后台核对对应版本的远端元数据并升级文件清单，不更新模型权重。

本机认证与输入安全

  • Doclib 增加持久化 Bearer Token 认证：SDK 默认发现本机服务时自动读取凭证；直接调用 HTTP 接口时，需要携带有效 Token。
  • 加强本机凭证保护：按平台设置并校验 POSIX 私有权限及 macOS、Windows ACL，限制其他账户访问或替换凭证文件。
  • 加强 URL 输入的 SSRF 防护：连接前校验域名解析得到的全部地址，并固定连接已校验的公网 IP，拒绝内网、回环、链路本地、云元数据及其他特殊地址。
  • 收紧 URL 输入下载行为：采用直连方式，不跟随重定向，不使用环境中的 HTTP/HTTPS/ALL 代理设置。
  • 执行文档资源限制：当文档解压或结构展开超出预算时终止处理，覆盖 OOXML 解压、XLSX 合并区域展开等场景；通过 resource_limit 返回明确错误，并保留 Tasks 的逐文件失败信息。

升级提示

  • 直接调用 Doclib HTTP 接口的客户端需要适配 Bearer Token 认证。 SDK 默认发现本机服务时自动读取凭证；显式指定服务地址或 Socket 路径时，需要同时提供 Token。
  • 依赖内网 URL、重定向或环境代理下载输入文件的部署需要调整输入方式。 内网文档可改为通过客户端上传。
  • 设备配置变更需要重启相关进程后生效。 运行设备确定后，在当前进程内保持稳定；ONNX 新增的独显显存门槛仅作用于自动模式。
  • 最低依赖版本更新为 DocVortex 0.6.21 和 ModelScope 1.39.1。

4.2.0

MinerU 4.2.0 upgrades WebUI interaction and source tracing, improves parsing of complex PDFs, formulas, and tables, and strengthens device selection, model caching, service management, and input security. These are cumulative changes since 4.1.0, including improvements released in 4.1.x.

WebUI interaction and source tracing

  • Bidirectional source navigation: Click a region in a PDF or image to locate and highlight its parsed content, or click a parsed paragraph, heading, formula, or figure to return to its source. Content merged across pages supports switching source pages.
  • Improved layout visualization: Merged paragraphs, lists, and tables can reference multiple source regions, with connecting lines to help inspect reading order, content ownership, and cross-page merges.
  • Result outline navigation: A collapsible heading outline provides quick access to document sections.
  • Redesigned uploads and controls: File selection, drag and drop, paste, file types, upload progress, and cancellation share one upload area. Compact tier and page-range controls improve selection and dragging, while showing the parsing service's CPU/GPU state.
  • Faster PDF previews and exports: Preview local PDFs directly in the browser while uploading, preserve the mounted viewer and zoom after parsing, and overlay layout annotations. Reuse prepared inputs, semantic results, and assets to avoid repeated PDF and preview generation.

PDF, formula, and table parsing

  • Improved inline formulas: Standard/Advanced use VLM recognition for inline formula regions outside tables in the native PDF text path, retaining local formula recognition when the result is empty or invalid.
  • Footnote formulas and page-edge regions: Preserve formula identity and reduce incorrect ownership between formulas, numbers, and body text.
  • Nested and overlapping regions: Reduce duplicate text and excessive merging, correct annotations inside figures, and improve the reading order of merged images.
  • Consistent layout detection: Restore per-class output thresholds in both ONNX and Torch.
  • Native text recovery: Improve character recovery with empty ToUnicode maps and remove residual Unicode private-use characters after post-OCR processing and fallback restoration.
  • Sheared body text: Preserve normal body text when every character in a line has a shear transformation.
  • Rotated tables: Account for page rotation when interpreting native text orientation.
  • Atomic table content: Keep images and formulas intact during table reconstruction rather than splitting them across cells.
  • Cross-page tables: Preserve text boundaries between joined cells and improve blank continuations, sparse blank rows, and shared row spans.

Native document parsing

  • Tagged PDF semantics: Flash native PDF text parsing can use Tagged PDF/PDF/UA structure and reading order, with geometric rules handling uncovered or untrusted content.
  • Paragraph continuation: Use indentation, sentence endings, and other page evidence to improve uppercase-leading continuations and paragraph merging across pages and columns.
  • Native tables: Correct row segmentation affected by unusual glyph bounds and row-span ownership in some numeric tables.
  • Font weight: Improve embedded-font weight interpretation to reduce normal body text incorrectly marked as bold.
  • Office images: Use placeholders for some images exceeding processing limits so that one image does not fail the whole document.

Device selection, tier recommendations, and diagnostics

  • Shared process device selection: Model loading, automatic tier recommendations, and llama.cpp automatic mode reuse the selected runtime device, reducing duplicate probes and differences between entry points.
  • Multiple GPUs: Match Windows GPU and memory information by physical PCI address.
  • Automatic tiers: Recommend tiers from the actual small-model device. GPUs can enable all tiers; CPUs enable Basic from 4 available logical cores and Standard from 12.
  • ONNX discrete-GPU selection: Windows/Linux automatic mode checks total dedicated memory. Direct GPU selection requires a capacity that rounds to at least 6 GiB; insufficient or unconfirmed capacity uses the CPU.
  • Runtime state: Service health can report initialized CPU/GPU state without triggering device selection.
  • Faster diagnostics and CLI startup: Run isolated runtime probes concurrently, stream reports by section, standardize capacity and device summaries, and retain full diagnostics and candidate reasons in JSON. Help and version commands load their entry points on demand.
  • Cleaner shutdown: Improve VLM shutdown and resource release.

Router and APIs

  • Dynamic upstream management: Query, register, and remove remote parsing nodes without restarting the Router.
  • Graceful removal: Stop assigning new work, then wait for existing tasks, transfers, and result references to finish.
  • Router cascading: Connect a downstream Router as an upstream using the existing V1 protocol and result leases.
  • Separate administration credentials: Authenticate dynamic node management independently from parsing requests.
  • Paths and filenames: Correct CLI detection of relative and hidden-file paths, and consistently reject path-like filenames and control characters in Tasks, Router, and synchronous uploads.

Model downloads and cache validation

  • File manifests: Record expected files and sizes for repository, directory, and single-file resources; detect missing or truncated files, broken links, and invalid manifests before reuse.
  • Offline reuse and incremental repair: Validate normal caches without network access or hashing large weights. Repair missing or incorrectly sized files while reusing valid files.
  • Legacy completion markers: Continue offline reuse after required-file and shard checks. When online, verify remote metadata for the recorded revision and upgrade manifests without updating model weights.

Local authentication and input security

  • Persistent Doclib bearer tokens: SDK default local discovery reads credentials automatically; direct HTTP calls require a valid token.
  • Credential protection: Set and verify POSIX private permissions and macOS/Windows ACLs.
  • URL SSRF protection: Validate all resolved addresses and connect to a validated public IP, rejecting private, loopback, link-local, cloud metadata, and other special addresses.
  • Direct URL downloads: Do not follow redirects or use HTTP/HTTPS/ALL proxy environment settings.
  • Document resource limits: Stop decompression or structural expansion beyond configured budgets, including OOXML archives and XLSX merged ranges. Return a clear resource_limit error and preserve per-file Tasks failures.

Upgrade notes

  • Direct Doclib HTTP clients must provide a bearer token. Default SDK discovery reads it automatically; explicit service addresses or socket paths require an explicit token.
  • Deployments using private URLs, redirects, or environment proxies for input downloads must adjust their input workflow. Upload private documents through the client.
  • Restart the relevant process after changing device configuration. Runtime device selection stays stable within a process; the new ONNX memory threshold applies only to automatic mode.
  • Minimum dependencies are DocVortex 0.6.21 and ModelScope 1.39.1.

Upgrade / 升级：pip install -U "mineru==4.2.0"

Full changes: https://github.com/opendatalab/MinerU/compare/mineru-4.1.0-released...mineru-4.2.0-released

roboflow/supervision ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.31.6supervision-0.31.6 Stable2026-11-10T11:12:15Z

v0.31.6: Key points on empty frames, zero-shot Transformers results, and drawing on grayscale and palette scenes

A fixes-only patch: fewer crashes on empty frames, more Transformers models that load, and annotators that draw on any scene.

  • KeyPoints filters stop raising TypeError on video frames with no people.
  • Grounding DINO and RF-DETR segmentation results load, and OWLv2 detections get real class names.
  • DetectionsSmoother keeps unconfirmed tracks as separate boxes instead of one averaged box.
  • Labels and images draw on grayscale, BGRA and Pillow palette scenes.
  • Integer boxes no longer wrap around, and narrow-integer mask shifts no longer crash.

It's a drop-in upgrade, but check the Migration guide if you use ViTPose class IDs, YOLO-NAS confidences or compare stored outputs.

✨ Spotlights / highlights

Key points survive empty frames

sv.KeyPoints.empty() now holds empty class_id, keypoint_confidence and detection_confidence arrays instead of None, and selecting key points from it returns an empty result. Pose connectors such as sv.KeyPoints.from_ultralytics return it for every frame without people, so the documented filters raised TypeError mid-video. ViTPose poses from sv.KeyPoints.from_transformers now all get class ID 0 instead of 0..N-1, and sv.KeyPoints.from_yolo_nas keeps each pose score as detection_confidence, so with_nms works on YOLO-NAS poses. (#2911, #2920, #2914, #2909)

import supervision as sv

key_points = sv.KeyPoints.empty()  # what pose connectors return for an empty frame
len(key_points[key_points.detection_confidence > 0.5])
# 0.31.6: 0  -> correct, an empty frame has nothing to filter
# 0.31.5: TypeError: '>' not supported between instances of 'NoneType' and 'float'
#         -> bug, the confidence arrays were None
key_points[:, [0, 1]].xy.shape
# 0.31.6: (0, 0, 2)  -> correct, no poses so nothing to select
# 0.31.5: IndexError  -> bug, selection crashed on an empty frame

Zero-shot and RF-DETR results from Transformers

sv.Detections.from_transformers now reads results of post_process_grounded_object_detection. Grounding DINO results used to raise AttributeError: 'list' object has no attribute 'cpu'; they now load with the matched phrases as class names and class_id=None. OWL-ViT, OWLv2 and OmDet-Turbo keep the text query index as class_id and take class names from text_labels; id2label=model.config.id2label used to name them LABEL_0, LABEL_1. RF-DETR instance segmentation results requested with return_binary_maps=True load too. If a result has no recognized fields, the error now points to the official rfdetr package, whose predict() returns sv.Detections directly. (#2913, #2912, #2921)

import supervision as sv

# results = processor.post_process_grounded_object_detection(...)[0]  # Grounding DINO
detections = sv.Detections.from_transformers(results)
detections.data["class_name"]  # matched phrases, e.g. ["a cat", "a dog"]
detections.class_id  # None

DetectionsSmoother keeps unconfirmed tracks apart

Detections with a negative tracker_id, which some trackers use for tracks they haven't confirmed yet, now pass through sv.DetectionsSmoother unchanged. They used to share one history and come back as a single averaged box. Frames where only some tracks keep oriented-box corners no longer raise a merge error. (#2926, #2923)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

smoother = sv.DetectionsSmoother()
detections = sv.Detections(
    xyxy=np.array([[0, 0, 10, 10], [100, 0, 110, 10]], dtype=float),
    tracker_id=np.array([-1, -1]),  # not confirmed by the tracker yet
)
smoother.update_with_detections(detections).xyxy.tolist()
# 0.31.6: [[0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 10.0], [100.0, 0.0, 110.0, 10.0]]
#         -> correct, two objects stay two boxes
# 0.31.5: [[50.0, 0.0, 60.0, 10.0]]
#         -> bug, both were averaged into one phantom box

Drawing on grayscale, BGRA and palette scenes

sv.RichLabelAnnotator annotates grayscale scenes, shaped (H, W) or (H, W, 1) or given as a Pillow grayscale image, and sv.draw_image draws on grayscale and BGRA scenes. Boxes and labels on Pillow palette images now come out in the colors you asked for, not black. Labels such as 0 or False show up as text instead of empty boxes. (#2916, #2906, #2907, #2892)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

scene = np.zeros((100, 100), dtype=np.uint8)  # grayscale frame
detections = sv.Detections(
    xyxy=np.array([[10.0, 10.0, 60.0, 60.0]]), class_id=np.array([0])
)
sv.RichLabelAnnotator().annotate(scene, detections, labels=["car"]).shape
# 0.31.6: (100, 100)  -> correct, grayscale scene annotated in place
# 0.31.5: TypeError: color must be int or single-element tuple
#         -> bug, grayscale scenes were not supported

🔄 Migration guide

Nothing was removed, renamed or deprecated, but a few fixes change what existing code returns:

  • Different values: ViTPose poses from KeyPoints.from_transformers all get class_id 0; use np.arange(len(key_points)) if you used it as a per-person index. (#2914) KeyPoints.from_yolo_nas(...).as_detections().confidence reports the model score instead of the mean key point confidence. (#2909) OWL-ViT, OWLv2 and OmDet-Turbo class names come from text_labels, even when you pass id2label. (#2913) xywh_to_xyxy returns int64 for integer input, or float64 when a corner exceeds the int64 range. (#2922)
  • Different rows and shapes: DetectionsSmoother returns unconfirmed detections as their own rows, after the smoothed tracks. (#2926) KeyPoints.empty() fields are empty arrays, not None. (#2911) With OpenCV, resize_image, scale_image and letterbox_image keep (H, W, 1) input as (H, W, 1). (#2905)
  • Different counts, colors and labels: PolygonZone no longer counts anchors in (-1, 0). (#2924) ColorPalette.from_matplotlib rounds colors like to_hex, so some move by one level. (#2903) Labels such as 0 or False, drawn as empty boxes before, now show their text. (#2892)
  • Without OpenCV: mask_to_polygons returns polygons in OpenCV's RETR_TREE order (#2900), image writes save most non-uint8 images instead of failing, though multi-channel non-uint8 TIFFs still fail (#2901), and HeatMapAnnotator colors move closer to OpenCV (#2904). Regenerate stored references made with the NumPy fallback.
  • New error: drawing on a Pillow palette image whose palette is full and has no opaque entry raises ValueError instead of drawing wrong colors. (#2907)

📝 Notable changes

🌱 Changed

  • Detections.from_transformers suggests the official rfdetr package when a result has no recognized fields, and its docs recommend rfdetr for RF-DETR models. (#2921)

🔧 Fixed

Key points

  • KeyPoints.empty() holds empty class_id, keypoint_confidence and detection_confidence arrays instead of None, so confidence filters no longer raise TypeError on frames without poses. (#2911)
  • Selecting key points from an empty result, as in key_points[:, [0, 1]], returns an empty result instead of raising IndexError. (#2920)
  • KeyPoints.from_transformers gives every ViTPose pose class ID 0; with the old 0..N-1 IDs, keypoint mAP against person targets scored too low and per-class EdgeAnnotator edges raised ValueError. (#2914)
  • KeyPoints.from_yolo_nas keeps each pose score as detection_confidence, so with_nms no longer raises ValueError on YOLO-NAS poses. (#2909)

Model connectors

  • Detections.from_transformers reads zero-shot results from post_process_grounded_object_detection: Grounding DINO no longer raises AttributeError, and OWL-ViT, OWLv2 and OmDet-Turbo take class names from text_labels. (#2913)
  • Detections.from_transformers reads RF-DETR instance segmentation results with return_binary_maps=True, pairing binary maps with segments_info by position; an empty (0, H, W) stack gives empty masks. (#2912)
  • Classifications.from_clip and Classifications.from_timm accept bfloat16 outputs, including mixed-precision inference, and return float32 probabilities for them. (#2894)

Tracking, zones and box math

  • DetectionsSmoother returns detections with a negative tracker_id unchanged instead of averaging them into one box; when those rows carry oriented corners, confirmed tracks without corner history get their smoothed axis-aligned bounds as corners. (#2926)
  • DetectionsSmoother returns all tracked boxes when only some smoothed tracks keep oriented corners, using smoothed axis-aligned bounds instead of raising a merge error. (#2923)
  • PolygonZone excludes negative subpixel anchors before rounding, so an anchor just outside the top or left edge is no longer counted as pixel zero. (#2924)
  • xywh_to_xyxy promotes integer coordinates before adding widths and heights: a uint8 box [200, 20, 100, 40] becomes [200, 20, 300, 60] instead of wrapping its right edge to 44. (#2922)
  • move_masks computes slice bounds without integer overflow, so narrow integer offsets work on larger frames and no-overlap shifts with narrow or unsigned integer offsets return empty masks instead of raising. (#2918)
  • JSONSink keeps an exception raised inside a with block when writing or closing the file also fails; the cleanup error is logged. (#2902)

Annotators and colors

  • RichLabelAnnotator annotates grayscale scenes, (H, W), (H, W, 1) or Pillow grayscale modes, instead of raising TypeError or ValueError. (#2916)
  • Annotators draw the requested colors on Pillow palette images (P, PA) instead of other colors or black. (#2907)
  • draw_image draws on grayscale and BGRA scenes instead of raising a NumPy broadcast error. (#2906)
  • LabelAnnotator and RichLabelAnnotator draw labels such as 0, np.int64(0), 0.0 and False as text instead of empty background boxes. (#2892)
  • ColorPalette.from_matplotlib rounds colors to 8-bit values as Matplotlib's to_hex does instead of truncating them. (#2903)

Images and the NumPy fallback without OpenCV

  • resize_image, scale_image and letterbox_image return (H, W, 1) for (H, W, 1) input on OpenCV installs too, and tint_image and grayscale_image accept (H, W, 1) images. (#2905)
  • Image writes without OpenCV, such as ImageSink.save_image and dataset exports, keep 16-bit PNGs and save a saturated 8-bit copy for JPEG, BMP and WebP, instead of raising TypeError or returning False. (#2901)
  • mask_to_polygons lists polygons in OpenCV's RETR_TREE order without OpenCV too, and one- and two-pixel contours are int32. (#2900)
  • HeatMapAnnotator colors without OpenCV follow OpenCV's float32 HSV-to-BGR arithmetic instead of coming out one level off. (#2904)
  • create_batches yields each full batch before consuming the next item, so stopping early leaves the rest of the source intact. (#2893)

📚 Docs

  • New how-to section on attaching tracker IDs back to key points in Track Objects. (#2896)

🏆 Contributors

  • Mohammad Hijjawi (@MohammadHijjawi97, LinkedIn) – drew falsy labels as text and rounded Matplotlib palette colors like to_hex.

anomalyco/opencode ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.2.7 Stable2026-11-10T10:37:15Z

Highlights

  • GitHub Copilot Auto: Pick Auto in the model picker when your Copilot account has an Auto pool, and Copilot's router chooses the model for each session at the Auto discount. If routing fails, OpenCode falls back to the account's default chat model. (#62997)
  • Side Panel Tab Shortcuts: Right-click a side panel tab and choose Close tabs to the right. ⌘W now closes side panel tabs wherever focus is, ⌥⌘W or Ctrl+Alt+W always closes the session tab, and ⌘⇧T reopens the last closed tab of either kind where it was. (#62922)
  • Mark Sessions Read or Unread: Open the menu on a tab, Home row, project row or inbox card and choose Mark as unread to keep its dot until you next visit. (#62889)

Breaking changes

@opencode/ai: ImageResponse.image is removed

This affects code that imports @opencode/ai directly. Read response.images[0] instead of response.image. (#63011)

SDK: fewer endpoints wait for plugins to load

On a Location that is still loading plugins, agent.list returns the agents loaded so far, and integration.list, usage.limits, vcs.init, rpc.call and artifact.* no longer wait up to 5 seconds. Entries and actions that a plugin provides appear when it finishes loading, so refresh on the matching *.updated event. Model, provider, generate.*, voice.* and websearch.query endpoints still wait. (#62796, #63040)

Core

Improvements

  • MCP Logins Stay Signed In: Remote MCP servers that rotate refresh tokens no longer sign you out when several clients refresh at once or the token endpoint fails for a moment. (#62827, #63012)
  • read accepts a negative offset to read from the end of a file, such as -50 for the last 50 lines. An offset past the end returns the last lines and the file's line count instead of failing. (#62147)
  • When read can't find a file, it suggests close names: different capitalization, another extension, a small typo, or the matching path inside the project. (#62147)
  • Shell results end with Exit code: N. Commands killed by a signal show the signal name, and timeouts say how long they waited and suggest a larger timeout. (#62141)
  • Prompt cache warming times its pings from the model's cache.ttl by default (interval: "auto"), or every 4 minutes without a TTL. Models whose cache outlasts the warming duration are no longer pinged. (#62831)
  • When MCP servers connect, disconnect or change tools, Code Mode sends the model a short list of what changed instead of the whole tool catalog. (#62176)

Bug fixes

  • GitHub Copilot session costs use the amount Copilot charged instead of the catalog estimate, and content-filter errors name the filter that blocked the response. (#62034)
  • GitHub Copilot picks up a new API endpoint after a plan change on its next model refresh, so you no longer have to sign in again. (#62171)
  • Remote MCP servers that rotate refresh tokens no longer log you out when the TUI, desktop app, background service or several projects refresh the same login at once. (#62827)
  • A temporary failure while refreshing an MCP OAuth token keeps your login for the next try instead of marking the server needs_auth. (#63012)
  • MCP tool calls no longer fail when the server's result doesn't match the tool's own output schema, so completed actions aren't reported as failed. (#61254)
  • MCP prompts stay available when refreshing a server's prompt list fails. (#62266)
  • With background_compaction on, a background summary no longer stops a running Claude Agent session mid tool call. (#63025)
  • After opencode auth logout on a .well-known integration, its remote providers and policies stop applying until you log back in. (#62760)
  • OpenCode Console workspace policies still apply when Console's instructions or skills fail to load. (#62702)
  • Cloudflare AI Gateway providers set up with only gatewayApiKey now send that key. (#62574)
  • A v1 model override that sets only some capabilities no longer resets the others, so catalog capabilities like PDF input are kept. (#62573)
  • A v1 provider config that sets both api and options.baseURL keeps options.baseURL. (#62570)
  • Legacy model references with an inline variant, such as "openai/gpt-5#high", keep the variant. (#62254)
  • A fresh install imports saved provider credentials from the legacy auth.json, and skips an invalid one with a warning instead of stopping setup. (#62568)
  • Cache warming stops for a session when a ping rebuilds the cache instead of reading it, counts its interval from when a request was sent, and no longer pings during compaction or after the session is deleted. (#62926, #62941, #62972)
  • Tokens and cost from session.generate requests, including cache warming pings, count toward the session's totals. (#63006)
  • Under zsh, a glob that matches nothing, like rm *.tmp, is passed through as bash does instead of stopping the command. (#62141)
  • Truncated shell output that starts mid-line drops the partial first line, so the "showing lines X–Y" notice is exact. (#62141)
  • On Windows, multi-line commands no longer lose everything after the first line with PowerShell 7 from the Microsoft Store. (#62522)
  • read no longer cuts an emoji in half when it shortens a long line. (#62575)
  • Generated session titles no longer include the model's commentary. Thanks @shanebishop1. (#62267)
  • Markdown agents whose description starts with a bracketed tag, like [Beta] Reviews code, now load. (#62250)
  • Markdown agent and command files that fail to load log a warning with the file path and frontmatter error. (#62464, #62251)
  • Markdown agent discovery skips node_modules. (#62571)
  • Failed plugin installs from git show git's own error instead of "An unknown git error occurred". (#62261)

TUI

Improvements

  • The update and confirm dialogs have buttons you can move between with ←/→ and Tab/Shift+Tab. (#62906)
  • The model picker no longer has a Usage action; use /usage instead. (#62927)

Bug fixes

  • Session exports are saved in the current working directory instead of the system temp folder. (#62253)
  • Typing a child session's exact ID in the open-session menu lists that session. (#62262)
  • The TUI follows the terminal's current light or dark mode instead of a cached palette. (#62248)

Desktop

Improvements

  • Right-click a side panel tab to Close tabs to the right. ⌘W closes side panel tabs wherever focus is, the new Close session tab command (⌥⌘W or Ctrl+Alt+W) always closes the session tab, and ⌘⇧T reopens the last closed side panel or session tab where it was. (#62922)
  • Added Mark as read and Mark as unread to the menus for tabs, Home rows, project rows and inbox cards, and Rename to inbox cards. (#62889)
  • The answer you pick in the question dock shows as a raised card. (#62896)
  • Inline rename and custom-answer fields no longer show a focus border. (#62919)

Bug fixes

  • On Windows and Linux, Ctrl+Shift+R toggles the review panel again instead of reloading the window. (#62567)
  • Closing the last side panel tab slides the panel shut. (#62758)
  • Selects, menus, tooltips and toasts opened from a dialog are reachable by screen readers. (#62913)
  • An error appears when a path can't be opened in its default app. (#62572)
  • Cut, Copy, Paste and Select All in the right-click menu follow the app language. (#62252)
  • Ukrainian tool titles use nouns. (#62263)
  • Scrolling titlebar search results no longer jumps back when late results arrive, the inbox no longer announces already-running sessions as working on load, and the quote toolbar comes back after Escape. (#62597)

SDK

Improvements

  • session.generate returns tokens and cost along with text. (#62835)
  • credential.updated includes the integrationID and credentialID of the login that changed. (#62762)
  • Predicate questions in client.experimental.generate.decisions accept optional criteria: { true?, false? }. (#62819)

Extensions

Improvements

  • ACP clients can redirect a running turn with the _session/steering extension, advertised as _meta.steering.supported in initialize. Thanks @smagnuso. (#54394)

browser-use/browser-use ↗ 5 releases · 2 stable · 3 prerelease
1.0.1browser-use 1.0.1 Stable2026-11-10T09:27:28Z

Fixes on 1.0.0.

  • MCP without Chrome: browser-use --cli-mcp on servers, containers and CI boxes now drives its own Chromium, installed on first use. When no browser can run, you get one clear error.
  • http_get in the CLI and MCP always fetches from your machine.
  • --mcp with allowed_domains can no longer be bypassed through a cloud browser.
  • Windows: piped CLI code and long MCP output are UTF-8.
  • Read-only HOME: the error names the env var to set.
  • timeout=: the final answer arrives before the deadline, and provider retries respect it.
  • 0.x feature detection: hasattr(browser_use, '<removed 0.x name>') returns False.
  • Isolation: each run gets its own browser-harness workspace.
uv add 'browser-use==1.0.1'

View release →

1.0.0browser-use 1.0: CDP is all you need Stable2026-11-10T02:04:48Z

<img src="https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/releases/download/1.0.0/browser-use-1.0-vs-0.x-bench.png" alt="browser-use 1.0 vs 0.x: higher score, cheaper, faster, 80% less code" width="100%">

−136,866 lines. 80% of the library deleted.

We started over. The agent now writes Python in one persistent REPL, straight on Chrome DevTools Protocol. No DOM serializer, no action schema, nothing in between. Simplicity wins: it is now the state-of-the-art web agent.

  ● task
  │
  ● model writes a cell          new_tab(url); bu.click(41); js(...)
  │
  ● REPL runs it on Chrome (CDP) → output back to the model
  │
  ✓ finish(result)

Same models: up to 33 points higher, 2.7–5.2x cheaper, 2–3x faster.

uv add 'browser-use==1.0.0'
export BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=bu_...    # cloud.browser-use.com/new-api-key

from browser_use import Agent

print(Agent('Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo').run_sync().final_result())

Pin with ==. We ship fast from here.

<details> <summary><b>Upgrading from 0.x, benchmarks, everything that changed</b></summary>

Need 0.x? pip install 'browser-use<1'

What changes by default

0.x 1.0
Model (llm=None) required bu-fast on the Browser Use gateway
Browser (Browser()) local Chromium a Browser Use Cloud browser; Browser('chromium') for a local one
Key one per provider one Browser Use key (BROWSER_USE_API_KEY, or browser-use login)
Mode ultrafast (accessibility-tree ids) on bu-fast / bu-ultrafast; mode='default' opts out
run() limits max_steps=500 max_steps=100, timeout=1700 s

Your own model: ChatOpenAI, ChatAnthropic, ChatGoogle, or any Pydantic AI model string (llm='groq:llama-3.3-70b-versatile').

Upgrading from 0.x

Read MIGRATING.md first. It lists every 0.x option and its 1.0 answer.

  • An option 1.0 cannot honor raises a TypeError naming it before any browser starts or anything is billed. No silent no-ops. 0.x tuning knobs 1.0 has no use for (use_vision=True, flash_mode, max_failures, ...) warn once and are ignored.
  • Every removed 0.x import (all module paths from 0.1 to 0.13, and every public name of a module 1.0 kept) raises an ImportError that names the replacement and links MIGRATING.md. except ImportError fallbacks still work.
  • ActionResult(include_in_memory=...) warns and is ignored: every tool result stays in the conversation.
  • Once configuration checks pass, run() does not raise: a rejected key or model, a browser that fails to start (cloud create 402/5xx/timeout) and failures inside the run all return status='error' with result.error, and a browser that did start is stopped. A missing key still raises before anything is created.

Behavior changes to check

  • Browser() is a cloud browser. Tasks on localhost or private IPs need Browser('chromium') (one warning says so).
  • Browser(local=True) now means Browser('chromium'). Your own open Chrome is Browser('chrome').
  • Browser('chrome') attaches only to a browser whose live DevToolsActivePort is in your own profile dirs. It no longer attaches to whatever answers on 127.0.0.1:9222.
  • run(max_steps=...) defaults to 100 (was 500). One step is one model request.
  • is_successful() is None when the agent gave no verdict. Use r.is_done() and r.success is not False for the 0.x default.
  • errors() returns only the error that ended the run. Recovered cell errors are in result.messages.
  • result.partial is set only by checkpoint(..., partial=True). To get one, name the flag in the task: "after each page, save the titles with checkpoint('titles.json', titles, partial=True)".
  • result.cost_usd covers model and web search charges, not the cloud browser or its proxy.
  • The workspace (default: a new temp dir browser-use-*) is kept after the run. A workspace= path is used as given (made absolute, symlinks not resolved), so result.downloads[0].relative_to(workspace) works.
  • prohibited_domains=['example.com'] also blocks its subdomains. With any domain rule set, every file: URL is blocked. BROWSER_USE_ALLOWED_DOMAINS is not read: Browser() raises while it is set.
  • The 0.x gateway models (bu-2-0, bu-1-0, bu-latest, ...) are rejected. BROWSER_USE_LLM_URL is not read: use ChatBrowserUse(base_url=...).
  • LangChain chat models raise TypeError. Azure reads AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY (not AZURE_OPENAI_KEY) and needs OPENAI_API_VERSION.
  • browser-use --mcp prints a deprecation notice and serves the --cli-mcp tools (browser_exec, browser_screenshot, browser_login). browser-use init was removed.
  • The bu and browser console scripts are gone (they broke uv tool install next to other tools). Use browser-use or browseruse.
  • Extras aws, oci, video, examples, eval, core, openai-tools, cli-oci are gone (pip warns and installs without them). anthropic, google, mcp, cli, all still resolve; Anthropic, Google and MCP are core dependencies.

Also new

  • Typed results: Agent(task, output_model_schema=MyModel) then result.structured_output.
  • Uploads and downloads on local and cloud browsers: inputs from workspace=, downloads in workspace/downloads and result.downloads. The agent uploads the file you named and never creates or overwrites it.
  • Secrets: sensitive_data= typed with type_secret() and redacted from cell output, checkpoints, tool results and the result. Not a sandbox; see the trust model in MIGRATING.md.
  • Domain restrictions (allowed_domains, prohibited_domains, block_ip_addresses) with the 0.x patterns, in every browser mode.
  • Web search (web_search=True), billed per search to your Browser Use key and added to result.cost_usd.
  • CLI and MCP: browser-use pipes Python to browser-harness; browser-use --cli-mcp runs the MCP server; browser-use skill install registers the agent skill.
  • Docker: browseruse/browseruse:1.0.0 and ghcr.io/browser-use/browser-use:1.0.0. Every image build is smoke-tested on amd64 and arm64 in CI, on pull requests too.

Reliability

  • Streamed Responses. Every OpenAI Responses request (the Browser Use gateway and api.openai.com) is streamed. A streamed turn gets 300 s to its first event, then runs while events flow, up to 930 s. Long bu-smart turns no longer end at 300 s.
  • One transient retry. A failure reported inside HTTP 200 (server_error, overloaded, request_timeout, upstream_stream_failed) or a stream that ends before its final event is retried once per run, like a 429 or 5xx.
  • Compaction. A Responses request over the context window compacts once and is sent again. Models with smaller windows (gpt-4o, 128k) compact before they overflow. A streamed compaction item is sent back exactly as a non-streamed response would carry it, and a 400 on it resends the request once without the item.
  • Many agents in one process. An Agent raises a low soft open-file limit to min(hard limit, 10240) (macOS defaults to 256). A connection error now names its cause, e.g. ModelConnectionError: Connection error. (OSError: [Errno 24] Too many open files).
  • Proxied gpt-6-luna. ChatOpenAI('gpt-6-luna', base_url=...) gets reasoning_effort='none' on any host, so tools work behind a proxy.
  • Pages with workers. wait_for_network_idle() no longer waits for Web Worker scripts and cross-site iframes, which ran it to its 10 s cap.
  • Dialogs. A navigation held by a beforeunload prompt, or a screenshot with a JS dialog open, names the dialog within seconds.
  • keep_alive. A run after a dead browser daemon continues in the agent's tab, not on about:blank.

Benchmark

BU Bench v2, 60 tasks x 3 reps, cloud browsers, 2026-11-09.

agent score [95% CI] $/task s/task
browser-use 1.0 (mode='ultrafast', the mode bu-fast uses) 62.5 [58.3, 66.5] 0.070 650
Browser Use Pi (e7007f2), same window 56.3 [50.2, 62.4] 0.082 743

An OpenAI outage hit this window. Pi lost 18 of 180 runs to provider errors, 1.0 lost 1 (its one retry recovered 13 of 14). So the raw gap overstates quality. With provider errors excluded, paired 1.0 minus Pi: score +1.1 [-3.2, +5.7] (no significant difference), cost -$0.013/task [-0.024, -0.004], time -114 s/task [-202, -31]. Same quality, cheaper and faster.

Both arms ran gpt-6-luna on api.openai.com, judged by gpt-5.6-luna (xhigh). The default bu-fast model on the Browser Use gateway was not part of this run. 95% task-clustered bootstrap intervals.

Known issues

  • Every dependency is pinned exactly (==), and the lockfile is committed. This is a supply-chain policy: you get exactly the versions CI tested. The cost: a project that needs a different version of pydantic, pydantic-ai-slim, mcp, google-genai, pillow or another pinned package cannot install 1.0 next to it. Tell us which pin blocks you.
  • google-genai stays below 3: 3.0.0 calls the Gemini API on v1, which rejects tools. This goes away once Pydantic AI supports it.
  • Viewport emulation does not work on cloud browsers yet. Use Browser('chromium') for another viewport.

Report problems at https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/issues

Full changelog: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/compare/0.13.14...1.0.0

</details>

View release →

1.0.0rc51.0.0rc5: release candidate for browser-use 1.0 Prerelease2026-11-09T23:36:28Z

Release candidate (second one published, after 1.0.0rc4). Install it explicitly. Plain pip install browser-use and uv add browser-use still give you 0.13.14 until 1.0.0 ships. Please report problems at https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/issues and say you are on 1.0.0rc5.

browser-use 1.0 replaces the engine. The agent no longer picks one action per step from a DOM dump. It writes Python in one persistent REPL with the browser-harness helpers and raw CDP, on Pydantic AI. The library went from 68,815 to 13,154 lines of Python. The 0.x surface you use most (Agent, Browser, Tools, run(), final_result()) still works.

Install

uv add 'browser-use==1.0.0rc5'
export BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=bu_...    # get one at https://cloud.browser-use.com/new-api-key

or pip install 'browser-use==1.0.0rc5'. CLI only: uv tool install 'browser-use==1.0.0rc5'. Python 3.11 or newer.

from browser_use import Agent

result = Agent('Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo').run_sync()
print(result.final_result())

What changes by default

0.x 1.0
Model (llm=None) required bu-fast on the Browser Use gateway

Mode ultrafast (accessibility-tree ids) on bu-fast / bu-ultrafast; mode='default' opts out

  • Domain restrictions (allowed_domains, prohibited_domains, block_ip_addresses) with the 0.x patterns, in every browser mode.

  • Docker: no image for release candidates. Build one from the tag: git clone -b 1.0.0rc5 https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use && cd browser-use && docker build . -t browseruse. Every image build is smoke-tested on amd64 and arm64 in CI, on pull requests too.

  • Streamed Responses. Every OpenAI Responses request (the Browser Use gateway and api.openai.com) is streamed. A streamed turn gets 300 s to its first event, then runs while events flow, up to 930 s. Long bu-smart turns no longer end at 300 s.

Benchmark

BU Bench v2, 60 tasks x 3 reps, cloud browsers, on this commit:

agent score [95% CI] $/task s/task
browser-use 1.0 (default, mode='ultrafast') 62.6 [58.2, 67.1] 0.071 585
browser-use 1.0, mode='default' 61.1 [56.5, 65.6] 0.073 675
Browser Use Pi (e7007f2), same window 61.3 [57.0, 65.4] 0.087 689

Paired, 1.0 default minus Pi: score +1.4 [-2.3, +5.0] (no significant difference), cost -$0.016/task [-0.027, -0.004] (18% cheaper), time -105 s/task [-167, -42] (15% faster). Every arm ran gpt-6-luna on api.openai.com, judged by gpt-5.6-luna (xhigh); 178 of 180 runs judged for the default arm, 180 for mode='default', 179 for Pi. The default bu-fast model on the Browser Use gateway was not part of this run.

95% task-clustered bootstrap intervals.

Telemetry

The library and the CLI send anonymized usage telemetry: counts, model and helper names, exception classes, hostnames of visited pages. Never the task, results, code, typed text, secrets or page content. No feature flags. Turn it off with ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY=false: no event, no background thread, no file written.

Fixed since 1.0.0rc4

  • A file downloaded in a second tab no longer makes Browser.stop() wait 300 s.
  • page_info() right after new_tab(url) no longer fails on local Chromium (CLI and MCP).
  • The CLI sends its saved telemetry backlog instead of dropping events.
  • Ctrl-C during browser-use login --device-code exits cleanly; clearer errors when no browser is found or Chrome exits at start.
  • Security: Browser('chrome'), the CLI and MCP no longer attach to whatever Chrome answers on port 9222; file: URLs are blocked under every domain rule; MCP http_get checks redirects against domain rules; the daemon folder must be private; page text is stripped of terminal escape codes in logs; Docker base images are pinned.
  • Install lines pin the exact version. Pin browser-use with ==, never >=: we move fast and releases can break things.

Known issues

  • Every dependency is pinned exactly (==), and the lockfile is committed. This is a supply-chain policy: you get exactly the versions CI tested. The cost: a project that needs a different version of pydantic, pydantic-ai-slim, mcp, google-genai, pillow or another pinned package cannot install 1.0 next to it. Tell us which pin blocks you.

  • The docs at docs.browser-use.com still show plain uv add browser-use / pip install browser-use, which install 0.13.14 during the release candidate. Use the install lines above.

  • google-genai is capped below 3: 3.0.0 calls the Gemini API on v1, which rejects tools. The cap goes away once Pydantic AI supports it.

  • Viewport emulation does not work on cloud browsers yet. Use Browser('chromium') for another viewport.

Full changelog: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/compare/0.13.14...1.0.0rc5

1.0.0rc41.0.0rc4: release candidate for browser-use 1.0 Prerelease2026-11-09T20:55:10Z

Release candidate (first one published; the version numbers 1.0.0rc1 to rc3 were unusable on PyPI). Install it explicitly. Plain pip install browser-use and uv add browser-use still give you 0.13.14 until 1.0.0 ships. Please report problems at https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/issues and say you are on 1.0.0rc4.

uv add --prerelease allow 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc4'
export BROWSER_USE_API_KEY=bu_...    # get one at https://cloud.browser-use.com/new-api-key

or pip install --pre 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc4'. CLI only: uv tool install --prerelease allow 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc4'. Python 3.11 or newer.
