  • The workspace (default: a new temp dir browser-use-*) is kept after the run. A workspace= path is used as given (made absolute, symlinks not resolved), so result.downloads[0].relative_to(workspace) works.
  • prohibited_domains=['example.com'] also blocks its subdomains. BROWSER_USE_ALLOWED_DOMAINS is not read: Browser() raises while it is set.
  • The 0.x gateway models (bu-2-0, bu-1-0, bu-latest, ...) are rejected. BROWSER_USE_LLM_URL is not read: use ChatBrowserUse(base_url=...).
  • LangChain chat models raise TypeError. Azure reads AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY (not AZURE_OPENAI_KEY) and needs OPENAI_API_VERSION.
  • browser-use --mcp prints a deprecation notice and serves the --cli-mcp tools (browser_exec, browser_screenshot). browser-use init was removed.
  • The bu and browser console scripts are gone (they broke uv tool install next to other tools). Use browser-use or browseruse.
  • Extras aws, oci, video, examples, eval, core, openai-tools, cli-oci are gone (pip warns and installs without them). anthropic, google, mcp, cli, all still resolve; Anthropic, Google and MCP are core dependencies.

Also new

  • Typed results: Agent(task, output_model_schema=MyModel) then result.structured_output.
  • Uploads and downloads on local and cloud browsers: inputs from workspace=, downloads in workspace/downloads and result.downloads. The agent uploads the file you named and never creates or overwrites it.
  • Secrets: sensitive_data= typed with type_secret() and redacted from cell output, checkpoints, tool results and the result. Not a sandbox; see the trust model in MIGRATING.md.
  • Domain restrictions (allowed_domains, prohibited_domains, block_ip_addresses) with the 0.x patterns, in every browser mode.
  • Web search (web_search=True), billed per search to your Browser Use key and added to result.cost_usd.
  • CLI and MCP: browser-use pipes Python to browser-harness; browser-use --cli-mcp runs the MCP server; browser-use skill install registers the agent skill.
  • Docker: no image for release candidates. Build one from the tag: git clone -b 1.0.0rc4 https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use && cd browser-use && docker build . -t browseruse. Every image build is smoke-tested on amd64 and arm64 in CI, on pull requests too.

Reliability

  • Streamed Responses. Every OpenAI Responses request (the Browser Use gateway and api.openai.com) is streamed. A streamed turn gets 300 s to its first event, then runs while events flow, up to 930 s. Long bu-smart turns no longer end at 300 s.
  • One transient retry. A failure reported inside HTTP 200 (server_error, overloaded, request_timeout, upstream_stream_failed) or a stream that ends before its final event is retried once per run, like a 429 or 5xx.
  • Compaction. A Responses request over the context window compacts once and is sent again. Models with smaller windows (gpt-4o, 128k) compact before they overflow. A streamed compaction item is sent back exactly as a non-streamed response would carry it, and a 400 on it resends the request once without the item.
  • Many agents in one process. An Agent raises a low soft open-file limit to min(hard limit, 10240) (macOS defaults to 256). A connection error now names its cause, e.g. ModelConnectionError: Connection error. (OSError: [Errno 24] Too many open files).
  • Proxied gpt-6-luna. ChatOpenAI('gpt-6-luna', base_url=...) gets reasoning_effort='none' on any host, so tools work behind a proxy.
  • Pages with workers. wait_for_network_idle() no longer waits for Web Worker scripts and cross-site iframes, which ran it to its 10 s cap.
  • Dialogs. A navigation held by a beforeunload prompt, or a screenshot with a JS dialog open, names the dialog within seconds.
  • keep_alive. A run after a dead browser daemon continues in the agent's tab, not on about:blank.

Benchmark

BU Bench v2, 60 tasks x 3 reps, cloud browsers, on this commit:

agent score [95% CI] $/task s/task
browser-use 1.0 (default, mode='ultrafast') 62.6 [58.2, 67.1] 0.071 585
browser-use 1.0, mode='default' 61.1 [56.5, 65.6] 0.073 675
Browser Use Pi (e7007f2), same window 61.3 [57.0, 65.4] 0.087 689

Paired, 1.0 default minus Pi: score +1.4 [-2.3, +5.0] (no significant difference), cost -$0.016/task [-0.027, -0.004] (18% cheaper), time -105 s/task [-167, -42] (15% faster). Every arm ran gpt-6-luna on api.openai.com, judged by gpt-5.6-luna (xhigh); 178 of 180 runs judged for the default arm, 180 for mode='default', 179 for Pi. The default bu-fast model on the Browser Use gateway was not part of this run.

95% task-clustered bootstrap intervals.

Telemetry

The library and the CLI send anonymized usage telemetry: counts, model and helper names, exception classes, hostnames of visited pages. Never the task, results, code, typed text, secrets or page content. No feature flags. Turn it off with ANONYMIZED_TELEMETRY=false: no event, no background thread, no file written. Every field is listed in TELEMETRY.md

Known issues

  • Every dependency is pinned exactly (==), and the lockfile is committed. This is a supply-chain policy: you get exactly the versions CI tested. The cost: a project that needs a different version of pydantic, pydantic-ai-slim, mcp, google-genai, pillow or another pinned package cannot install 1.0 next to it. Tell us which pin blocks you.

  • The docs at docs.browser-use.com still show plain uv add browser-use / pip install browser-use, which install 0.13.14 during the release candidate. Use the install lines above.

  • A file downloaded in a second tab the agent opened makes Browser.stop() wait 300 s before it returns. The file itself is complete. Fixed in the next rc.

  • CLI and MCP on a local Chromium: print(page_info()) right after new_tab(url) can fail with Cannot read properties of null. Call wait_for_load() in between. Agent runs recover on their own. Fixed in the next rc.

  • google-genai is capped below 3: 3.0.0 calls the Gemini API on v1, which rejects tools. The cap goes away once Pydantic AI supports it.

  • Viewport emulation does not work on cloud browsers yet. Use Browser('chromium') for another viewport.

Full changelog: https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/compare/0.13.14...1.0.0rc4

View release →

1.0.0rc21.0.0rc2: release candidate for browser-use 1.0 Prerelease2026-11-09T16:52:16Z

Release candidate (first one published; the 1.0.0rc1 version number was unusable on PyPI). Install it explicitly. Plain pip install browser-use and uv add browser-use still give you 0.13.14 until 1.0.0 ships. Please report problems at https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use/issues and say you are on 1.0.0rc2.

browser-use 1.0 replaces the engine. The agent no longer picks one action per step from a DOM dump. It writes Python in one persistent REPL with the browser-harness helpers and raw CDP, on Pydantic AI. The library went from 68,815 to 13,154 lines of Python. The 0.x surface you use most (Agent, Browser, Tools, run(), final_result()) still works.

Install

uv add --prerelease allow 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc2'
uv run browser-use login    # one key: default model, cloud browser, CLI and MCP

or pip install --pre 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc2'. CLI only: uv tool install --prerelease allow 'browser-use>=1.0.0rc2'. Python 3.11 or newer.

from browser_use import Agent

result = Agent('Find the number of stars of the browser-use repo').run_sync()
print(result.final_result())

What changes by default

0.x 1.0
Model (llm=None) required bu-fast on the Browser Use gateway
Browser (Browser()) local Chromium a Browser Use Cloud browser; Browser('chromium') for a local one
Key one per provider one Browser Use key (browser-use login or BROWSER_USE_API_KEY)
Mode ultrafast (accessibility-tree ids) on bu-fast / bu-ultrafast; mode='default' opts out
run() limits max_steps=500 max_steps=100, timeout=1700 s

Your own model: ChatOpenAI, ChatAnthropic, ChatGoogle, or any Pydantic AI model string (llm='groq:llama-3.3-70b-versatile').

Upgrading from 0.x

Read MIGRATING.md first. It lists every 0.x option and its 1.0 answer. Need 0.x? Pin it: pip install 'browser-use<1'.

  • An option 1.0 cannot honor raises a TypeError naming it before any browser starts or anything is billed. No silent no-ops. 0.x tuning knobs 1.0 has no use for (use_vision=True, flash_mode, max_failures, ...) warn once and are ignored.
  • Every removed 0.x import (all module paths from 0.1 to 0.13, and every public name of a module 1.0 kept) raises an ImportError that names the replacement and links MIGRATING.md. except ImportError fallbacks still work.
  • ActionResult(include_in_memory=...) warns and is ignored: every tool result stays in the conversation.
  • Once configuration checks pass, run() does not raise: a rejected key or model, a browser that fails to start (cloud create 402/5xx/timeout) and failures inside the run all return status='error' with result.error, and a browser that did start is stopped. A missing key still raises before anything is created.

Behavior changes to check

  • Browser() is a cloud browser. Tasks on localhost or private IPs need Browser('chromium') (one warning says so).
  • Browser(local=True) now means Browser('chromium'). Your own open Chrome is Browser('chrome').
  • run(max_steps=...) defaults to 100 (was 500). One step is one model request.
  • is_successful() is None when the agent gave no verdict. Use r.is_done() and r.success is not False for the 0.x default.
  • errors() returns only the error that ended the run. Recovered cell errors are in result.messages.
  • result.partial is set only by checkpoint(..., partial=True). To get one, name the flag in the task: "after each page, save the titles with checkpoint('titles.json', titles, partial=True)".
  • result.cost_usd covers model and web search charges, not the cloud browser or its proxy.
  • The workspace (default: a new temp dir browser-use-*) is kept after the run. A workspace= path is used as given (made absolute, symlinks not resolved), so result.downloads[0].relative_to(workspace) works.
  • prohibited_domains=['example.com'] also blocks its subdomains. BROWSER_USE_ALLOWED_DOMAINS is not read: Browser() raises while it is set.
  • The 0.x gateway models (bu-2-0, bu-1-0, bu-latest, ...) are rejected. BROWSER_USE_LLM_URL is not read: use ChatBrowserUse(base_url=...).
  • LangChain chat models raise TypeError. Azure reads AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY (not AZURE_OPENAI_KEY) and needs OPENAI_API_VERSION.
  • browser-use --mcp prints a deprecation notice and serves the --cli-mcp tools (browser_exec, browser_screenshot). browser-use init was removed.
  • The bu and browser console scripts are gone (they broke uv tool install next to other tools). Use browser-use or browseruse.
  • Extras aws, oci, video, examples, eval, core, openai-tools, cli-oci are gone (pip warns and installs without them). anthropic, google, mcp, cli, all still resolve; Anthropic, Google and MCP are core dependencies.

Also new

  • Typed results: Agent(task, output_model_schema=MyModel) then result.structured_output.
  • Uploads and downloads on local and cloud browsers: inputs from workspace=, downloads in workspace/downloads and result.downloads. The agent uploads the file you named and never creates or overwrites it.
  • Secrets: sensitive_data= typed with type_secret() and redacted from cell output, checkpoints, tool results and the result. Not a sandbox; see the trust model in MIGRATING.md.
  • Domain restrictions (allowed_domains, prohibited_domains, block_ip_addresses) with the 0.x patterns, in every browser mode.
  • Web search (web_search=True), billed per search to your Browser Use key and added to result.cost_usd.
  • CLI and MCP: browser-use pipes Python to browser-harness; browser-use --cli-mcp runs the MCP server; browser-use skill install registers the agent skill.
  • Docker: no image for release candidates. Build one from the tag: git clone -b 1.0.0rc2 https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use && cd browser-use && docker build . -t browseruse. Every image build is smoke-tested on amd64 and arm64 in CI, on pull requests too.

A streamed compaction item is sent back exactly as a non-streamed response would carry it, and a 400 on it resends the request once without the item.
  • Many agents in one process. An Agent raises a low soft open-file limit to min(hard limit, 10240) (macOS defaults to 256). A connection error now names its cause, e.g. ModelConnectionError: Connection error. (OSError: [Errno 24] Too many open files).
  • Proxied gpt-6-luna. ChatOpenAI('gpt-6-luna', base_url=...) gets reasoning_effort='none' on any host, so tools work behind a proxy.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v15.15.1 Stable2026-11-10T07:06:39Z

No replay after an outage

When the worker was down or refused a capture, some capture paths kept that work and sent it again later, which could start model calls nobody asked for. They now drop what the worker refused and carry on with new work (#5663):

  • Transcript capture: a transcript line the worker refuses or cannot take is skipped. Before, the watcher stopped at that line and, once the worker was back, sent it again with every line after it. A restart no longer resends lines from an outage either.
  • Cowork: a prompt, observation or summary that fails to send is dropped instead of being spooled and sent again by a later hook. Only session start, session end and subagent stop events are still spooled, and prompts, observations and summaries left in an older spool are removed on its next flush.
  • OpenCode: a session start the worker refused is sent once, like observations and summaries, instead of being retried in the background for about 10 seconds.

Fixes

  • Redaction: Grafana service account tokens (glsa_), Grafana Cloud tokens (glc_) and Postman API keys (PMAK-) are redacted, by the worker and by the Cowork shim. Turn either off by name with CLAUDE_MEM_REDACT_DISABLED_BUILTINS (grafana_token, postman_key) (#5662, thanks @kevin9327).
  • OpenClaw: <private> content is removed from tool output before the output is cut to 1,000 characters. Before, a cut inside a private block dropped its closing tag, so the private text was stored. Text after an unclosed <private> tag is now dropped too (#5663).
  • Status and doctor: claude-mem status names every dependency in words, such as "Observer data directory setup required" instead of observer_dir: setup_required, and its hint points to claude-mem doctor instead of a Settings screen that does not show these. doctor shows a status from a newer worker instead of dropping it (#5661).
  • Work state: Done and Dropped close an item in any letter case, everywhere. Before, saving an owner-goals item as Done was refused although every reader already treated it as closed (#5661).
  • CCS Align: a corrupt exclusion file is refused instead of being read as empty, so excluded observations stay excluded and the file is not overwritten (#5663).
  • Viewer: observations of type sensitive get their own "Sensitive" filter on the session page instead of being counted under "Other" (#5663).

Also in this release

  • Setup and My Work: each setup, billing, scan and refresh state is defined once and shared by the installer, the worker and the pages, so a new state fails the typecheck until every screen handles it (#5661).
  • Tests: about 60,000 lines of tests removed: tests that only checked their own fixtures, duplicate cases (folded into tables) and four duplicate copies of the agent-cost-report Python tests. Kept regression tests run production code (#5663).
  • CI: Bun is pinned to 1.4.4, ripgrep is installed, the Cowork runner and agent-cost-report Python tests run, and tool-heavy suites moved to the integration lane (#5663).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.15.0...v15.15.1

View release →

v15.15.0 Stable2026-11-10T02:20:40Z

Critical fix

  • The worker no longer freezes on its first image (#5601): a worker started from a plugin root with no node_modules (the Claude Code marketplace clone) could freeze for good. Bun's auto-install loaded sharp's wasm build, whose first call blocks Bun's main thread, so timers and /api/health stopped and hooks kept reclaiming the worker. Now every worker, server and plugin spawn runs bun --no-install, the installer starts the worker from the plugin cache copy where it just ran bun install, and the media converter refuses sharp's wasm build before decoding, so a worker started some other way reports decoder_unavailable instead of freezing (#5653). Thanks to @vieGPT for the report and to @Kirat0201 for reproducing it and proposing the approach.

New

  • One Rules + Tools line at session start: the SessionStart header (project and time, Mode, Legend, Format, Fetch, Stats) is now one line, Access Claude-Mem Rules + Tools: ~/.claude-mem/RULES-AND-TOOLS.md. That file holds the format, legend, mode, how to fetch details, the work-state rule and memory-use guidance, and is rewritten only when its text changes. The terminal banner now ends on a closing line with the viewer link and how many timeline lines are shown (#5654).
  • Savings settings removed: the context header no longer prints a savings line, so CLAUDE_MEM_CONTEXT_SHOW_SAVINGS_AMOUNT and CLAUDE_MEM_CONTEXT_SHOW_SAVINGS_PERCENT and their viewer toggle are gone. Settings files that still carry them load as before (#5654).

Fixes

  • Installer: the setup state is prepared before the browser opens, so a quick first choice is no longer erased and the installer no longer waits for a choice you already made (#5631, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Context: the prior-session reply shown at startup is read from the end of the transcript instead of loading the whole file, so long sessions start faster and very large transcripts keep that context (#5625, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Terminal banner: when the banner cut lands exactly on a line boundary, the whole line that fits is kept, and the line count also counts rows in the plain block format (#5654).
  • Tests: the server's semantic context route probes the embedding model only where pgvector exists, so CI no longer times out on a cold model download (#5659).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.14.0...v15.15.0

View release →

v15.14.0 Stable2026-11-09T22:27:23Z

New

  • Focus areas in My Work: My Work now opens on an overview of your focus areas. Each area gets a card with bundled concept artwork, its goal and live request counts; open one to see its requests. The setup reveal screen uses the same view. Artwork can be set to full, compact or off, and the images ship with the plugin, so nothing is generated or fetched at runtime (#5652).
  • claude-mem can update itself: the worker checks once a day for the release promoted in releases/promoted-release.json, and installs only a newer version that npm shows as published by the release workflow with provenance, only while you have been away for a while. A failed update puts the previous version back. npx claude-mem doctor shows an Auto-update row. Turn it off with CLAUDE_MEM_AUTO_UPDATE=false; Claude Code's own update switches (DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER and friends) also turn it off. New installs register the marketplace with Claude Code's background update off, so only one updater runs. See the new auto-update docs page (#5500).
  • Startup context is the timeline alone: the SessionStart message is now the header lines followed by the timeline, ending on the newest entry. The longer work-state and memory-use instructions moved to the claude-mem MCP server's instructions. The terminal banner has the same shape (#5487).
  • Faster hooks: hooks run from a small hook-only bundle instead of the full worker bundle. One hook takes about half the time and memory; a dozen parallel hooks use about a quarter of the CPU (#5541, thanks @bulanovk).
  • Raw tool evidence search (opt-in, local): with CLAUDE_MEM_TOOL_USES_SEARCH_ENABLED=true, a local search with deep: true looks through stored tool input and output when the observation search finds nothing, and returns #T IDs for get_tool_uses (#5167, thanks @ShitK).
  • Korean UI: the viewer, My Work, setup and Observation TV can be shown in Korean (#5522, thanks @BaeJinSeop).

Privacy and security

  • Context: privacy filters now also apply to prior-session replies shown at startup (#5626).
  • Backups: SQLite snapshots are created readable only by you (#5642).
  • Media: cloud uploads stop starting after you opt out (#5622).
  • Redaction: cookie header values are redacted, matching the Cowork shim (#5591, thanks @DeryFerd), and the Cowork shim now redacts the key shapes it still sent verbatim (#5543, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Worker: read routes that resolve a project from cwd are guarded (#5590, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Auth: stored access tokens that are not strings are rejected (#5506, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Launcher: macOS signing is verified before the launcher is put in place (#5503, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Telemetry: a saved consent choice is kept when identifiers are created (#5629).

Installers and integrations

  • Atomic config writes: Codex, OpenCode, OpenClaw, Kimi, Antigravity, Hermes, Grok Bot and OMP config and hook files are written atomically, so an interrupted install can't leave a broken file (#5592, #5593, #5594, #5595, #5596, #5597, #5598, #5599, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Cursor: uninstall removes the hooks and MCP server that install --ide cursor adds (#5584, thanks @tanishqbaweja).
  • Installer: tool version probes, browser-setup worker requests and npm child processes are bounded, and npm descendants are terminated on timeout (#5634, #5638, #5644).
  • Installer: existing host installs are found in their configured folders, non-executable paths are skipped during runtime discovery, Roo Code is detected by its marketplace ID, and a compatible tree-sitter on PATH is used during repair (#5552, #5556, #5560, #5576, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Hooks: hooks dispatch through the installed plugin resolver (#5504, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • OpenCode: one bounded live observer handoff (#5621). Pi: tool results are normalized for evidence (#5605). Goose: executable paths are quoted in the YAML config (#5606). Devin: only its own tool-map entries are translated (#5512, thanks @Kirat0201). Telegram: sends are bounded (#5636).
  • Adapters: explicit tool failures are kept as evidence (#5620).
  • DSH: worker MCP error results are passed through and failures during response body reads are classified correctly (#5604, #5619).

My Work

  • Lists are ordered by original activity time, owner goals replay in event order, and conversation titles are scoped to their platform (#5580, #5589, #5561).
  • Stale group evidence is removed when conversations move, invalid observation file entries are skipped, Git branch products are attributed to confirmed remote updates, and deleted Windows temp folders are recognized (#5587, #5585, #5533, #5554).
  • Large histories and catalogs stay under SQLite's limits, deeply nested publishing results are handled, ledger snapshots use isolated atomic writes, and deliverable revisions that share a short hash are kept (#5588, #5549, #5579, #5582, #5536).
  • The empty Rules page explains that it is waiting for an agent, the first live request is announced after an empty list, and setup memory search cards show the recorded agent (#5575, thanks @Kirat0201; #5564, #5574).

Search, context and memory

  • Smart search bounds source memory and accepts portable path filters; file-history ranking and index pages are faster (#5534, #5586, #5602).
  • Search keeps multiline titles in one table row, keeps zero-valued date bounds, propagates SQLite failures before replacing corpora, and keeps distinct DSH results (#5583, #5520, thanks @Kirat0201; #5610, #5548).
  • Context selects equal-time rows consistently, enforces mode filters on the server, respects a literal ~ and +-prefixed filenames in shell reads (#5608, #5623, #5633, #5510, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Knowledge corpora keep source evidence and identities, overlapping operations can't overwrite saved state, and cancelled queries are not saved (#5528, #5612, #5648).
  • Smart navigation shows Rust traits, implementations and data definitions (#5531).
  • Dedup reads are bounded and the IDF model is rebuilt from a locked snapshot (#5632, #5613).
  • CLAUDE.md honors a zero observation count and claude-md clean keeps authored indentation (#5508, thanks @Kirat0201; #5566).

Backup, restore, import and sync

  • Restore refuses newer-schema snapshots and remote-worker restores before touching live data, reports committed swaps despite cleanup errors, keeps damaged recovery pairs, and protects the selected restore point from retention (#5649, #5562, #5624, #5630, #5532).
  • Backups account for WAL pages on the snapshot volume (#5627).
  • Import keeps observation provenance and reinforcement history, accepts a UTF-8 BOM, indexes legacy lists consistently and exits nonzero when rows are rejected (#5609, #5518, #5514, #5516, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Sync removes replica vectors after remote tombstones, refreshes vector metadata after project remaps, quarantines out-of-range numbers, rejects malformed ports and protects excluded folders in stale marketplace directories (#5647, #5645, #5611, #5573, #5525).
  • Chroma persists checkpoints for prototype-named projects (#5607). Notion exports stay within UTF-8 request limits (#5529). Memory export keeps its deadline through the response body (#5547).

Server and CLI

  • Server: generation completions are metered once, reasoning corrections stay within the job's paid-send cap, operator generation state changes are locked, legacy ingestion routes get usage guards, event polling stops on disconnect, shutdown cleanup finishes after errors, and queue health reads are bounded during Redis outages (#5640, #5615, #5650, #5641, #5651, #5628, #5617).
  • PostgreSQL: explicit TLS policy is kept, failed pool connections recover, idempotent writes skip inference, redelivered events are linked, and scoped job counts are not compared to global totals (#5637, #5537, #5538, #5546, #5559).
  • CLI: completed generation jobs are not retried, key rotation stops when the previous key lookup fails, command-owned job pools close, signal-terminated children report failure, and search can query a worker without a local plugin (#5603, #5618, #5639, #5569, #5568).
  • MCP: the npm MCP server launches in the invoking checkout, an empty explicit port is rejected, server MCP clients don't see unavailable local operations, and setup prints the server MCP endpoint (#5553, #5571, #5551, #5563).
  • SQLite handles are closed when initialization fails; rejected worker run promises are observed; transient media cloud reads stay retryable (#5635, #5616, #5614).
  • Smaller fixes: legacy SQLite timestamps show the right year, Unicode survives prompt-history shortening, single-quoted skip reasons are accepted, the Pro trial pitch is skipped for accounts already on trial or Pro, log tails stream correctly, and folder context generation reports incomplete work (#5557, #5550, #5558, #5545, #5526, #5565, #5572).

Docs

  • Translated READMEs have fixed links and headings (#5527, thanks @vietmy1711), and two old release notes now describe cloud sync and telemetry correctly (#5577, thanks @Kirat0201).

Thanks to @Afloat16 for the large majority of the fixes in this release.

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.13.0...v15.14.0

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.66 Stable2026-11-10T05:33:36Z

omo thread says when a broken install leaves it on this device only. A thread client sidecar that cannot be loaded no longer makes omo thread read <alias> silently answer not_found: stderr names the sidecar, the error, and the reinstall step. The CLI sign-in's own warnings (a retired trust pin, a failed device-service forget) now reach stderr too. (#12936)

A message to a busy thread now reaches the turn it is running, by default. thread_send and thread_handoff with no delivery mode (and omo thread send without --mode) steer the recipient's running turn and start a turn when it is idle, on this device and on another device alike; a message queues behind the running turn only when the caller asks for follow_up, or when the turn cannot take a steer (a waiting question, a compaction, an engine that reports no turn epoch). A send that steers a turn ending at that very moment falls back to the lane the target has then instead of failing. (#12921, part of #12879)

OmO runs on senpi 2026.11.9-2. A tool that an eval cell awaits no longer leaves the running cell blank for a moment when it finishes: its streamed lines stay until the cell's own output or its result replaces them (senpi#6257). (#12920)

omo remote send now travels the same road as a session's thread_send to another device, and every reply from another device is explained by one mapping. The person at a terminal who sends to a session on another device goes through the relay transport (the same offline check, the same steer preflight against the running turn, a receipt in this device's gateway store) while keeping their own prompt record; what the user reads when a command on another device is refused (omo remote, the attach TUI, the thread tools and the SDK) comes from a single module, so a device that did not answer, a missing grant or an uncertain result reads the same everywhere. (#12911, part of #12879, fixes #12885)

OmO runs on senpi 2026.11.9. It brings senpi's admit_external_message (a message relayed from another device's session is admitted as an ordinary delivery) and makes a relayed delivery read exactly like a same-machine one, with the device kept in its metadata only. The one-channel relay transport (#12879) needs both. (#12904)

The Install button on omo.dev's header no longer presses against or slips past the pill's right edge. The floating nav bar gives its last control the same inset as the logo on the left, and below 1000 px the section links move into the menu so nothing is pushed outside the pill in any language. (#12893)

A thread an agent creates or forks with thread_create now starts in the creator's own approval mode on this device too, not the host's default. A session created on another device already did (#6189); the two legs now run one body: a name is checked against the device the thread lands on (a name that device already lists live is name_conflict there), opening a thread that is live on another device answers that thread without opening it again, and the created thread's facts (name, working directory, timestamps) come from the engine's reply on both devices. (#12891, part of #12879)

Stopping, closing, renaming, re-modelling, changing the reasoning level of, or reading a thread works the same way whether that thread runs on this machine or on another device of your account. thread_set_reasoning now reaches a thread on another device too, and a session with no file yet that another client still holds answers the same on both. (#12890, part of #12879)

A message an agent sends to a session on another device now arrives there exactly as a message between sessions on one device. The receiving session gets a delivery entry (never a user message) with the same sender line and authority notice a same-device delivery carries, deduplicated under the same key, and it wakes on receipt the same way; a reply is just another send back. Nothing in the delivered text names the device it crossed, and omo remote send from a terminal is unchanged. A send from omo thread send to a session on another device is refused as data (the engine admits a command-line delivery only from its own machine) and points at omo remote send. (#12889, part of #12879, fixes #12882, fixes #12877, fixes #12346, fixes #12865, fixes #12857)

Sending a message to another session now goes through one delivery pipeline whether that session is on this machine or on another device. The device difference is a transport module behind one port; nothing a user sees changes in this release. (#12887, part of #12879)

A message to a session on another device now reads exactly like one from a session on the same machine. The receiving agent sees which session sent it, not which device, and replies by that session as usual. (#12878)

A subagent whose model takes more than 90 s to start answering is no longer aborted by runtime fallback. With runtime_fallback enabled, the first-prompt watchdog cut every subagent that showed no progress within a fixed 90 s and re-prompted the fallback model, so reasoning-heavy models that legitimately think longer before their first token were killed and their prompt was sent twice. The watchdog now waits 300 s, the same stream-start bound the engines use, and runtime_fallback.first_prompt_timeout_seconds sets it (0 disables it). (#11893, fixes #11887)

Fixed

  • omo thread send to a thread alias held on this device now reaches that thread when run from another workspace, the same way omo thread read does, instead of answering that no visible thread matches. (#12860)
  • Memory shutdown drain: the session-shutdown wiring no longer claims senpi puts no bound on its handler. senpi bounds one session_shutdown handler with its own warn threshold, per-handler timeout and total quit budget (shipped defaults 2 s / 10 s / 2 s, each user-tunable and 0-disableable), and the 1500 ms drain budget is sized under those defaults and pinned by a test that reads them from the installed senpi, so a host release that lowers a default below the drain fails there. No host cap was added from OmO and nothing bypasses the host abort; a journal flush skipped by the budget still raises its error-level alarm. (#12795, refs #10336)
  • The browser skill's docs and examples teach BrowserSkill 0.4 shared control for the omowright it now ships (^0.3.0): every gated page action carries the context of a fresh session.cooperate("sync"), and a cooperation_sync_required refusal waits for the person with session.cooperate("wait"), then re-syncs and replans. They also note which calls are gated, that a gated action throws a TypeError on an unknown option key (with the two calls that pass stray keys through instead), and that page.on("dialog") listeners receive a Dialog they can answer. The omowright this release ships includes running-browser discovery through DevToolsActivePort, goto on sparse and frameset pages, UTF-8 decoding across BrowserSkill IPC chunks and unique-only stale-ref recovery (thanks @ashmoonori-afk, code-yeongyu/omowright#115 to #118), and agent tabs in their own window (thanks @devxoul, code-yeongyu/omowright#120). (#12494)
  • The senpi-task cold-revive residency test is now bounded by the product's own liveness rules instead of a flat 20 s total (Part of #10993). A Windows child that blocks for a while during startup no longer fails it unless the product would also give up, and a real stall fails with the last stage it reached and the stall length. The test's child also reports senpi's startup phase timings, so the next block names its phase. (#11706)

Changed

  • Skills: ulw-research and mass research in mass-ulw now require every conclusion to rest on something a tool actually observed. Workers tag claims OBSERVED: and dead ends PROBED:, anything known only from memory is sent back as a lead, and mass-research verify nodes re-open each observed source. Both research editions state the rule in their opening contract, and every mass-ulw node prompt carries it in its VERIFY item. (#12832, fixes #12830)

<!-- macos-install-note -->

macOS install note

The app isn't signed with an Apple Developer ID yet, so macOS may call a downloaded build "damaged" — it's Gatekeeper quarantine, not a bad file. After moving the app to Applications, clear the flag once, then open normally:

xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine "/Applications/OpenHands Agent Canvas.app"

Signing + notarization is tracked in OpenHands/OpenHands#16774.

esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2.59.0Reasonix CLI v2.59.0 Stable2026-11-10T04:24:06Z

CLI archives built from the Studio release studio-v2.59.0.

View release →

studio-v2.59.0Reasonix Studio v2.59.0 Stable2026-11-10T04:20:48Z

本版改为全屏设置页、统一界面图标、默认开启跨项目任务派发，修复思考计时、下拉框与工具注册表卡死等问题，并移除安卓版。

变更

  • 设置改为全屏界面，左侧固定导航、右侧单列卡片铺满可用宽度；各分类的设置直接展开，通过胶囊定位，保留正在编辑的内容，并区分页面、卡片和字段标题（#15050）。
  • 侧栏底部保留带文字的「设置」和「发送反馈」入口；已开启的快捷入口集中在图标与名称并列的卡片中，支持悬停、点击和键盘操作，沿用显示开关与排序（#15050）。
  • 会话间消息与跨项目任务派发默认开启；显式关闭的用户配置继续保留（#15066）。
  • 界面中文改用随包的 Noto Studio 字体，不再落到系统字体：Windows 上不再由只有常规和粗两档的微软雅黑渲染，500 和 600 字重在中文上也能呈现层次（3844e7e55）。

修复

  • 发送、停止和浏览器导航按钮统一使用标准图标，修正停止方块过小的问题；手机端使用明确的停止符号，并正确弱化不可发送状态，外部打开按钮更易辨认（#15050）。
  • 导航栏选中态改为主题浅底色、清晰图标和轻边框，修复主题包下突兀的实心色块（#15050）。
  • 修复更新说明的语言按钮显示 settings.text 的问题，正确显示「简体中文」和「English」（#15050）。
  • 修复语言等下拉框的文字偏上问题，让文字与箭头垂直对齐（#15050）。
  • 切换模型或重载扩展后，仍可派发任务、打开项目并接收配对会话的回信（#15066）。
  • 优化模型选单的拥挤布局：模型名单独一行，协议和「读图」标签放在下一行，连接状态保持完整；长服务商地址不再撑出横向滚动条（#15040，关联 #15012）。
  • 合并工作台新标签页中重复的「资源管理器」和「文件…」入口；打开资源管理器即可直接搜索文件（#15040）。
  • 修复模型服务设置中容量数值、继承提示及计费方式被裁切的问题；价格输入框与币种选择框统一高度，按实际可用宽度和字号调整列数，铺满表单区域。订阅计费选项使用简短名称，并保留完整说明（#15054）。
  • 修复任务完成后旧思考卡片仍显示「思考中」并持续计时的问题。工具开始调用时停止思考计时，同一段回复中的交错事件不再拆出遗留卡片；后台会话确认结束前会先处理缓冲文字，重新打开会话时保留已记录的耗时（#15057）。
  • 将界面中的字符、emoji 和通用手绘图标统一为 Lucide 图标，覆盖导航、设置、模型菜单、工具卡片、附件、预览和窗口操作。搜索、读文件、列目录、等待、终止、编辑与删除使用对应的图标；自定义服务和打开程序的入口不再使用首字母占位，并统一图标尺寸与对齐（#15063）。
  • 修复对话向下滚动、展开的「执行过程」标题吸在顶部时，背景是透明的，导致标题和下面滚上来的执行内容叠在一起、两行文字重叠显示的问题：吸顶的标题现在用对话区的底色盖住它下面的内容（#15044）。
  • 修复设置里的下拉框（比如「按用途选择模型」里的「使用模型」）选中很长的名字时，文字自动换成好几行、溢出下拉框的问题：现在名字保持在一行里，放不下就截断，不再撑出框外（#15041）。
  • 修复设置、模型服务、用途选模型等页面里所有下拉框中，已选的文字偏向框的上沿、没有在垂直方向居中的问题（Windows 和 macOS 上都出现过）：现在文字在框里上下居中（#15041）。
  • 修复技能目录里的文档文件（比如 skills/某技能/README.md、skills/某技能/子目录/README.md）被误报成"没有描述，会加载但不会出现在技能索引里"的警告弹窗：这些嵌套的文件本来就不会被当成技能，现在只对真正保留下来的技能说这句话（#15045）。
  • 修复工具注册表的读锁被重复获取、在另一处同时注册或移除工具时可能整体卡死的问题；此前启用 MCP 或延迟加载能力的会话偶尔会一直显示「任务正在运行」（发现于 d09a7c557c 的测试）。

移除

  • 移除安卓版：发布页不再提供 ReasonixMobile-<版本>.apk，已安装的安卓应用不会再收到更新（dcee062）。

<!-- lang:en -->

Changed

  • Settings fills the application window with fixed desktop navigation and full-width cards in one column. Category controls stay expanded, pill navigation preserves drafts, and page, card and field headings have distinct levels (#15050).
  • The sidebar footer keeps labelled Settings and Send feedback rows. Enabled shortcuts appear in an icon-and-name card available by hover, click and keyboard, preserving saved visibility and order (#15050).
  • Session messaging and cross-project task dispatch are enabled by default; explicit user opt-outs are preserved (#15066).
  • Chinese text in the interface now uses the bundled Noto Studio face instead of the system font. On Windows it is no longer drawn in Microsoft YaHei UI, which has only regular and bold, so the 500 and 600 weights show on Chinese text too (3844e7e55).

Fixed

  • Send, stop and browser navigation controls use standard icons with balanced sizing. Stop keeps the same square on phones, disabled Send looks inactive, and opening a page externally has a clearer icon (#15050).
  • Selected navigation items use a subtle theme tint, readable icons and a light border instead of a solid accent block in theme packs (#15050).
  • Release-note language buttons display their language names instead of the untranslated settings.text key (#15050).
  • Text in language and other drop-down controls aligns vertically with the arrow (#15050).
  • Keep cross-project dispatch, project opening and paired replies available after model switches or extension reloads (#15066).
  • Model names have their own line; protocol and image badges sit below without squeezing connection status. Long provider endpoints stay within the menu (#15040; refs #15012).
  • The workbench's duplicate Explorer and Find a file entries are merged. Opening Explorer focuses the file search (#15040).
  • Provider settings display complete capacity values, inheritance hints and billing choices. Price inputs and currency pickers share a consistent height, and their columns fill the form while adapting to its width and text size. Subscription billing uses a shorter option label with the full explanation underneath (#15054).
  • Completed thoughts no longer keep counting as if they were still running. Thinking stops when a tool call begins, interleaved events keep one response together, and background panes drain buffered text before adopting an idle status. Reopened sessions retain the recorded duration (#15057).
  • Replaced character, emoji and hand-drawn interface icons with Lucide across navigation, settings, model menus, tool cards, attachments, previews and window controls. Search, file reads, directory listings, waiting, stopping, editing and deletion now have distinct semantic icons. Custom services and program launchers use icons instead of initials, with consistent sizing and alignment (#15063).
  • When an open "Work" heading stuck to the top while the conversation scrolled, its background was transparent, so the rows scrolling beneath it showed through and two lines of text overlapped. The stuck heading is now painted in the conversation's own colour (#15044).
  • A long chosen name in a Settings drop-down, such as the model in "Choose a model for each purpose", wrapped over several lines and spilled out of the box. It now stays on one line and is cut off when it does not fit (#15041).
  • The chosen text in every drop-down in Settings, model services and the per-purpose model table sat against the top edge of its box instead of the middle, on Windows and macOS alike; it is now centred vertically (#15041).
  • Documentation files inside a skill folder, such as skills/some-skill/README.md or one a level deeper, no longer raise the warning that a skill "has no description: it will load but won't appear in the skills index". Such nested files are never loaded as skills; the warning now appears only for a skill that is kept (#15045).
  • The tool registry no longer takes its read lock twice while building a turn's tool list. A concurrent tool registration or removal could deadlock it, leaving sessions with MCP or deferred capabilities stuck on "task running" (found by the test in d09a7c557c).

Removed

  • The Android app is discontinued: releases no longer carry ReasonixMobile-<version>.apk, and installed copies receive no further updates (dcee062).

What's Changed

  • fix(studio): improve model layout and merge duplicate file entry by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15040
  • fix(studio): expand settings and clarify navigation controls by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15050
  • fix(studio): size provider fields to their available space by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15054
  • fix(studio): settle thinking clocks at response boundaries by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15057
  • fix(studio): replace character icons with semantic Lucide icons by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15063
  • fix: enable cross-project dispatch and retain runtime bindings by @esengine in https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/pull/15066

Full Changelog: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/compare/studio-v2.58.1...studio-v2.59.0

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

NousResearch/hermes-agent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.23.1Hermes Agent v0.23.1 Stable2026-11-10T01:42:15Z

Hermes Agent v0.23.1

Release Date: November 9, 2026

Patch release. This tag rolls up the ~618 PRs merged since v0.23.0 into a stable release for the GitHub release (CLI and installers), the Microsoft Store app, Docker and Hermes Cloud. Full curated notes for this window ship with v0.24.0.

What this patch ships for plugin users: since the plugin catalog's site deploy on November 9, the published plugin-catalog.json was larger than the 2 MiB cap clients accept, so every install silently fell back to the catalog it shipped with. hermes plugins install <name> could not find plugins added since your last update, installed the pin your release shipped instead of the current one, and missed newer removed entries. This release raises the cap to 16 MiB, so the live catalog is used again (#151860, fixes #151856, thanks @100yenadmin). Installs that stay on v0.23.0 or older keep falling back until they update.

<!-- HERMES_BUILDS_TABLE -->

About this release

Measured at commit 3b421578588, the window since v0.23.0 contains 2,578 non-merge commits across 2,781 changed files (+151,690 / −62,595), 618 merged PRs and 409 closed issues.

Also in the window, undocumented here on purpose: Esc backtrack in the TUI and classic CLI (replacing the rewind list picker), group chats that run on a gateway without Desktop and can seat bots from other computers, saved gateway connections shared by Desktop and the Webapp, signing the in-app browser in with your real browser profile and filling saved logins from its address bar, hermes gateway attach for local clients, hermes clipboard image --json, new plugin API (session-scoped environment values, plugin JSON-RPC methods and events, llm.oneshot auxiliary tasks, a compression-stable conversation_id, an on_session_open hook in the gateway, a post_curator_run hook), faster transcript and handoff reads, and a large batch of new plugin-catalog entries.

Full curated release notes for this window ship with v0.24.0, which will document everything from v0.23.0 onward — highlights, feature areas, and complete contributor credits. Nothing in this window is skipped.

Updating

  • Microsoft Store app: updates through the Store.
  • Desktop app installed from the installer (.exe / .dmg): no change in this release; it updates with the next bundled release.
  • CLI: hermes update, or re-run the installer one-liner.
  • Docker / Hermes Cloud: nousresearch/hermes-agent:stable (or :latest).

Full Changelog: v0.23.0...v0.23.1

blader/humanizer ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.0Humanizer v4.0.0 Stable2026-11-10T01:35:15Z

Humanizer 4.0.0 lets the skill add a specific detail where the text is generic, and it rewrites the skill's own instructions and examples to be clearer.

A rewrite may now contain a detail that was not in your text. If you rely on the old rule, stay on v3.1.0.

What changed

  • The skill no longer forbids adding facts. Where the text is generic, it now adds a specific detail it knows to be true. It still keeps every claim the original made and treats a lost or changed claim as an error. This reverses the no-invention rule from 2.9.0. Patterns 14 and 17 follow the same change.
  • Reworked 15 "After" examples in SKILL.md and both README examples. Several were vaguer or stiffer than the text they replaced, used phrases from the skill's own watch lists, or changed a stated fact.
  • File mode now reads the whole current file before editing, even one written in the same session. Its summary says how many sections it reviewed, so a partial pass is visible (#326, #327).
  • Text being edited can contain fake closing tags or notes from an "operator" or a "system message". The skill now treats those as material to edit, never as instructions (#317).
  • Rewrote unclear sentences in SKILL.md and README.md. Coined terms such as "riders" and "shape edits" became plain words, and sentences that only announced or repeated a neighbor are gone. Step 3 now checks for dropped or changed facts first. Pattern 15 is now "Shallow -ing phrases". The README gives /humanizer as the only command (#330).
  • Added a blank line above each pattern label in SKILL.md so GitHub shows every label and example on its own line (#329).
  • The skill description no longer cites Wikipedia. The credit stays in the Source section and the README (#321).
  • Added a pi package manifest, so pi install git:github.com/blader/humanizer installs the skill (#315).
  • Removed the $schema key from the Claude plugin manifest, which caused a sync warning (#307).

Thanks to @marlandoj, @mathewgeejo, @AYastrebov, @thanhnguyentung95, @devilliersdutoit, and @Josephqaz for the fixes and reports in this release.

v0.42.0 Stable2026-11-10T00:37:19Z

New Features

  • Added saved flows (experimental): record a browser task once, then replay its requests. A run tries direct HTTP first, then the same request from the page, then a replay of the recorded commands, and saves the tier that worked. Runs send requests only to public addresses, and saving waits for in-flight requests and follows redirects to the end (#2236, #2397, #2432, #2433, #2444)
  • MCP tools now list only the shared arguments each tool uses (#2311)

Bug Fixes

  • Fixed session reuse and relaunch, now decided in one place in the daemon. A connection target is kept only after an attach succeeds, and security settings persist across daemon restarts (#2396, #2418, #2429)
  • Fixed Safari and iOS WebDriver sessions being dropped (#2385)
  • Fixed fill and type altering literal text, and mouse and element coordinates losing fractional values (#2394, #2393)
  • Fixed percent-encoded proxy credentials not being decoded (#2389)
  • Fixed downloads moving local files from an attached Chrome (#2392)
  • Fixed MCP tool results that repeated JSON stdout (#2390)
  • Fixed unknown commands and cookie operations, now rejected, and misplaced global options in batch commands (#2383, #2386)
  • Fixed errors output to print page error text, and labeled storage clear as storage rather than the request log (#2427, #2428)
  • Fixed macOS Chrome discovery for apps installed in the user's Applications folder (#2388)
  • Fixed Windows recording opening ffmpeg console windows (#2382)
  • Skipped a repeat Browser.getVersion call after the outdated-browser check (#2420)

Improvements

  • The core skill now tells agents to prefer snapshots: screenshot only when asked or for content the DOM cannot represent, and wait and snapshot again when an expected element is missing (#2453, #2461)
  • The package now declares a Node 22 minimum and drops engines.pnpm (#2404)
  • Docs and dashboard chat use openai/gpt-6-luna-fast (#2440)
  • Upgraded the repository to TypeScript 7

Fission-AI/OpenSpec ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.17.0v1.17.0 - Safe upgrades, 2.0 notice Stable2026-11-09T23:46:31Z

What's New in v1.17.0

openspec update now upgrades a project from any older release in one run, and announces that command files go away in OpenSpec 2.0.

Heads-up: command files (/opsx:*) go away in OpenSpec 2.0. Your OpenSpec skills stay and do the same work. To switch now, run openspec config set delivery skills && openspec update. The plan is in #2022.

New

  • 2.0 notice - While a configured tool still gets command files, openspec update ends with a short notice that they go away in 2.0, plus the one command to switch early. It stays silent once you use skills only, and for tools without command files, such as Codex.

Improved

  • Upgrades from any release - openspec update now upgrades a project made by any release since 0.12 in one run, without stopping to ask for --force. It removes old OpenSpec files nobody edited, including leftover copies that made some tools show every skill twice, and keeps any file you changed.
  • Safer cleanup - Cleanup never touches files outside your project, even when a folder inside it links somewhere else, and never removes anything when a tool fails to set up.

Fixed

  • Switching to skills only - openspec config set delivery skills followed by openspec update no longer deletes command files you edited. Only files OpenSpec generated are removed; edited ones are kept and listed.
  • Windows installer - irm https://openspec.dev/cli/install.ps1 | iex no longer fails with unsupported CPU on Windows PowerShell 5.1 machines that report no CPU architecture.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.16.1...v1.17.0

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v18.18.6 Stable2026-11-09T23:30:49Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Fixed

  • LiteLLM lists only the models the configured key can call (its authenticated /v1/models), keeping the proxy's metadata for them; a proxy whose /v1/models fails now lists no models instead of every model it serves (#18601)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • In Tern, a turn's long-press menu on a phone can edit and resend a prompt, retry the turn, open the rewind page on it, or fork the session from it into a new session
  • A rewind from the transcript says how many turns it dropped and offers Undo on its toast in Tern, which puts the turns and your draft back

Changed

  • Browser role/ selectors in Chrome tabs now match the role and name Chrome's accessibility tree reports, so they reach open shadow roots and skip hidden elements; in Tern every name observe() reports selects its element again (#18632 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • A late usage-limit error from a previous provider no longer blocks or cools down the selected provider; when the old provider has spent its retry budget, a replay-safe turn still tries the selected model without the old quota delay (#17098, #17571 by @Bensonluo)
  • Multi-question ask forms in Tern show Submit on the last question tab, not only on the Review tab
  • MCP headers and stdio env values that use !command now run again on a 401/403 (the request is retried once), on /mcp reconnect and other reconnects, and on /mcp reload, so servers whose command mints short-lived tokens keep working past expiry instead of staying 401 until restart (#18631)
  • Pausing an unapproved plan no longer tells the agent that the draft was approved or to continue executing it, and pausing with /plan closes an open plan review so it can no longer save and execute the plan (#18610).
  • Turning off lsp.enabled now also stops write and edit from starting language servers for diagnostics or formatting; previously only the lsp tool was disabled (#18205, #18490 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Fixed background tabs omp borrows (relay app.target, app.cdp_url) ignoring omp's clicks and keys or timing out on hover and drag; the page reads as visible while omp drives it and hidden again once released (#17831 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed the transcript jumping at the screen's top edge while scrolled back during a streamed reply, and the screen dropping two rows when a reply finished (#12307, #16117 by @galtamam-cmyk)
  • Focusing a subagent or returning to the main session no longer loses or freezes the next assistant reply when a fast tool turn finishes before the previous reply's links finish resolving, and messages and tool runs that arrive during the switch show exactly once, in order (#18564 by @eggpeat)
  • omp gc --strip now keeps every response of the latest replay turn (including earlier tool-call steps joined by their tool results), reference-only images, and the transcript's file permissions (#18636 by @eggpeat)
  • Session files whose entries were written without ids are re-indexed when loaded, and migrating old sessions no longer gives two entries the same id (#18636 by @eggpeat)
  • wait and write proc://<id>/kill now say how a settled job's result reached the caller: delivered by the call itself (it finished while the call ran), handed over by this snapshot (it superseded the job's queued completion push, so no duplicate follows), or genuinely late — the last keeps its "not auto-delivered; recovered by this snapshot" warning and adds how long before the call it had finished (#16052, #18635 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Fixed ask notes added with n right after picking an answer landing on the previous question instead of the one shown; a Tern click then Note still notes the answer just clicked, and the Note button names it (#18317 by @DonMikone)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Fixed

  • Fixed GTK4 apps such as Files (Nautilus) on X11: element clicks and computer.elementAt() now find their elements where they are on screen, and ax() no longer marks their enabled controls (disabled) (#18643 by @will-bogusz)
  • grep no longer searches the file its own output is redirected into, nor standard input redirected from that same file: both are refused with input file is also the output, as GNU grep does (#14808, #16489 by @yuzu-octopus)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • The wait result's JobSnapshot now carries settledAtMs (epoch ms when the run settled, absent while it is still running), so a snapshot can tell a result that finished during the call from one recovered from an earlier, never-delivered completion (#18635 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Added a thinking.set composer action so Tern's phone effort menu can pick a thinking level in one tap
  • Added All, Running, Done and Parked status scopes with counts to the agent hub's roster, so Tern's phone can filter agents by status
  • A prompt and a turn's answer list their actions for a phone's long press (actions.hold): Copy, Edit & resend (a prompt's), Retry turn, Rewind to here… and Fork from here; the desktop keeps its hover toolbars
  • A click or tap on a turn on the native rewind page picks it, and the rewind selector can open on a given turn (startAt)
  • A status notice can carry a button on its native toast (StatusNoticeAction), such as the Undo after a rewind

Fixed

  • Fixed streamed rows being clipped above the screen before they reached scrollback while the working row was up, so a scrolled-back terminal saw rows jump at the screen's top edge and the whole screen dropped when a run ended (#12307, #16117 by @galtamam-cmyk). Composer.setRuntimeChildren takes a billedChrome option for roots billed at their current height instead of through the floor.
  • Fixed rows of a streaming reply, and the top rows of a table taller than the screen, going missing from both the screen and scrollback with tui.completeScrollback on; such a table now keeps the column widths it had when it outgrew the screen (#16355 by @galtamam-cmyk)

@oh-my-pi/pi-wire

Added

  • actions.hold (TspMenuEntry[]): what a long press on a touch screen offers, beside actions.menu; and toast's action / label props for a button on the toast

  • fix(coding-agent): keep late quota errors off the fallback provider by @Bensonluo in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/17571
  • fix(builtins): refuse to grep the file grep writes its output to by @yuzu-octopus in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16489
  • fix(mcp): re-ran command-backed headers on 401 and reconnect by @roboomp in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18633

v18.18.4 Stable2026-11-09T18:57:58Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added the base64SkipThoughtSignature compat flag so Gemini gateways that decode thoughtSignature as base64 (such as the Hyperspace AI LLM proxy) can receive the unsigned-tool-call bypass sentinel without rejecting the request with a 400 (#18586 by @tuandinh0801).

Changed

  • Providers paused by a disabled auth rule (currently Google Antigravity) are left out of the built-in login list, refuse login, token refresh and usage probes, resolve no key from any source, and refuse requests and image generation with the pause reason; stored logins are kept and work again once the rule is removed, and an extension that registers its own login under the paused id takes it back (#18622 by @H4vC)

Fixed

  • Exclusive tools (edit, write, eval, …) called as Cursor MCP tools now keep their place in the tool batch, so a shell or read sent with an edit no longer runs against the old file or starts before an earlier call finishes (#18502 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Otari gateway provider: /login otari with OTARI_API_KEY, models discovered live per workspace (#17841 by @tbille)
  • Added the base64SkipThoughtSignature Google compat flag, which sends the Gemini unsigned-tool-call bypass sentinel base64-encoded for gateways that decode thoughtSignature as bytes (#18586 by @tuandinh0801).
  • Added a disabled "<reason>" auth rule that pauses a provider: no login offers it and nothing calls it, while its code and users' stored logins stay in place (#18622 by @H4vC)

Changed

  • Paused Google Antigravity, since Google may restrict accounts that use it outside its own apps (#18622 by @H4vC)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • SDK: PromptOptions.followUps queues prompts to run after the prompt, in order; if the prompt never reaches the agent they come back with it in DroppedPrompt.followUps

Changed

  • The task tool no longer advertises a per-call model field unless task.perCallModel is on or the bundled seance is spawnable; a model sent anyway is still refused before dispatch (#18554 by @xiangnan0811)

Fixed

  • /usage, session statistics, and omp stats now include session title spend from both the default fork title and the tiny title model, and tiny-model titles count toward --max-cost (#18177, #18491 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Retries after --model pi/<role> (or @<role>, <role>) now walk that role's retry.fallbackChains entry instead of the default chain, including on --continue (#18389, #18534 by @harshaygadekar)
  • Fixed a -> / => list sent to an idle session running its second item before its first; if the first item can't start (Esc, a refused or failed setup), the whole list goes back to the editor (#10802)
  • Fixed auto-compaction repeating every turn when the system prompt and tool schemas alone exceed the compaction budget; it now warns once instead (#18589).
  • Fixed a blank per-call model ("" or whitespace) failing the gate for task calls, eval agent() and eval workpool() instead of using the agent's configured model (#18576 by @iwangjie)
  • task tool calls with an empty model string or empty model array now use the agent's normal model routing (#18554 by @xiangnan0811)
  • Fixed todo append without a phase failing instead of adding tasks to the active phase, the last phase, or a new Tasks phase (#17265, #18575 by @taotao7).
  • Learned-only memory guidance no longer tells the model to read a missing memory_summary.md (#18523 by @nick-maderight).
  • A wait that times out now says how long it waited and how long a back-to-back wait allows, instead of returning a bare still-running snapshot that agents read as an empty result, and the back-to-back reminder tells agents not to start sleep jobs to wait longer (#18612 by @alphastorm).
  • auth-gateway stdio now follows a role's configured fallback chain even when modelRoles does not set the role (a chat role without its own chain inherits the default chain) (#18509 by @enieuwy)
  • In the Pseudo shell visualization, a ;-separated chain no longer draws its statement separators as rows of their own, so echo a; echo b reads as two statements (#18258, #18497 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Autoresearch update_notes with append_idea now creates an ## Ideas section instead of inserting the bullet before the first heading when notes contain only a similarly named heading such as ## Ideas backlog or ### Ideas (#18505 by @eggpeat)
  • Exclusive tools (edit, write, eval, …) called as Cursor MCP tools now keep their place in the tool batch, so a check sent right after an edit sees the new file and an edit no longer starts before an earlier read finishes (#18502 by @eggpeat)
  • Quarantined Hindsight memories are now deleted once their 30-day review period ends even when no new memory is quarantined (#18504 by @eggpeat)
  • The compaction display summary no longer requests reasoning, so thinking models return a summary instead of spending the 512-token cap on reasoning and returning nothing (#18541 by @phillipxkang)
  • Retries on the same model now stop after three consecutive generic aborts instead of spending the whole retry budget (#18535 by @nick-maderight)
  • lsp.formatOnWrite no longer re-indents 2/4-space files at 1 space per level when they contain JSDoc/block comments or one-off odd indents, and no longer converts tab-indented files that open with a JSDoc block to spaces (#18544)
  • Isolated task sandboxes now finish cleanup after a SIGHUP or other signal even when releasing them takes longer than the agent shutdown grace period (#18519).

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed late background embedding writes overwriting newer vectors for updated memories or recreating vectors for deleted memories (#18503 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed a second Mnemopi instance configured for a different local embedding model silently reusing the first instance's encoder while stamping its vectors with the configured model: local encoders are now cached per model, and a failed load evicts only that model's entry (#18562 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Fixed

  • Fixed omp stats re-parsing every unchanged session after a filesystem's device number changes, such as across ZFS remounts (#14736) (#18536 by @Alex-Wengg).

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • ask HTML forms with two or more <fieldset>s show one tab per question plus a Review tab in Tern; Next moves on, ←/→ and PgUp/PgDn switch tabs, and Submit lives on Review (#18608 by @Gabriel-Hiss)
  • ask HTML forms hide to one row with app.ask.hide (Alt+H) or the Hide button, as the ask dialog does (#18608 by @Gabriel-Hiss)

Fixed

  • Prefilled single-line ask fields now open at the start of their value instead of scrolling to the end when focused (#18585).
  • Fixed the │ rails of a wrapped box-drawing tree line (├── **label**: …) taking the color and weight of the node's leading bold, italic or code span under colorful Markdown; continuation rails now match the first row's rails and the node text keeps its own styling (#18584 by @chan1103)
  • Fixed relayed Windows terminal events after an event-loop stall so they neither abort the running turn nor type TSP data into the composer (#18512).
  • Fixed transcript rows a few turns above the composer disappearing from the screen and from scrollback, at a place that moved with every resize, while a todo list or subagent panel stayed up after a turn (#18510 by @mccraigmccraig)
  • Fixed xd://<device>:raw and xd://<device>:conflicts resolving as that device. Those suffixes stay in the name, so the read or write no longer runs the device (#18561 by @wisehead)
  • Fixed ask HTML forms taller than the composer area being cut off and unscrollable in Tern: the form now scrolls in the composer's place, and Cancel/Submit stay visible
  • Fixed the Agent Hub in Tern freezing itself and the session behind it once hundreds of agents are listed: moving the selection no longer re-sends every roster row, and live roster updates on large hubs are batched (up to once a second) so the terminal keeps up

v2.14.9v2.14.9 — Skills load for judgement calls too, even with many other skills installed. Stable2026-11-09T21:54:36Z

Skills load for judgement calls too, even with many other skills installed.

  • 50 skills (patch bump each): the "or asks" part of each description now covers the usual judgement calls in its area: choosing between two options ("which promise to lead with", "screen recording or AI avatar"), whether something is okay or fair ("is it fair to say a rival lacks offline mode", "is this markup allowed"), and whether it's enough ("are six directory listings enough"). Lower-value list items were cut to pay for it, so the pack stays at 18,662 characters and all 53 skills keep their descriptions with 90 other skills installed. On judgement-style prompts nobody read while writing the change, the right skill was picked for about 90% (92.5% twice, 89.6% for the shipped wording, from 66.5%) with 90 other skills installed and 95.3% (from 85.8%) with the pack alone, with no false triggers. programmatic-seo, revops, and signup are unchanged.
  • Trigger eval: a fourth held-out set (heldout4.json, judgement-style), used unread to judge this change.

v2.14.8v2.14.8 — Skills now load for advice and judgement questions, not just requests to make something. Stable2026-11-09T21:13:32Z

Skills now load for advice and judgement questions, not just requests to make something.

  • Every skill (patch bump each): each description now names the questions the skill answers ("or asks whether a result is real, when to stop a test, or which version won") next to what it makes. Descriptions that only said "write", "plan", or "create" lost questions like "should we say they lack SSO?" or "where would you look for buyers?": the model answered from general knowledge instead of loading the skill. Total 18,676 characters (from 19,259), so all 53 skills keep their descriptions even with 90 other skills installed (2 dropped before). On a judgement-style test set nobody read while writing the change, the right skill was picked for 87.7% of prompts (from 73.6%) with the pack alone and 73.6% (from about 60%) with 90 other skills installed. Advice-style prompts with other skills installed went from about 84% to 94%, and the original set reaches 100% alone.
  • Trigger eval: two more held-out sets (heldout2.json advice-style, heldout3.json judgement-style), seven heldout.json prompts now include the material they mention, and missed rows record the model's reply. The README covers run-to-run noise (about plus or minus 4 points on crowded runs) and when to use --repeat.

v2.14.7v2.14.7 — social (2.4.3 → 2.5.0): new references/ship-post.md for build-in-public posts that start… Stable2026-11-09T20:03:36Z
  • social (2.4.3 → 2.5.0): new references/ship-post.md for build-in-public posts that start from the commit log (closes #833, by @thiguillaumat-art). It reads the git log and diff since a ref, asks the developer one question (what was annoying or impossible before, and what's different now), picks the one user-visible change, leads with the user's pain rather than the implementation, and drafts one X post and one subreddit post. Hard rules: no number that isn't in the commits, the diff or the developer's answer; no claims about user behaviour nobody reported; flag a user sentence the code doesn't support; drafts only; say so when the week was only chores. SKILL.md links it next to the No AI Tells section, and the description gains a build-in-public trigger. New evals (ids 11–14).

v2.14.6v2.14.6 — The ads operating check now defines "normal variance" as the trailing 6-8 period range, s… Stable2026-11-09T19:58:38Z

The ads operating check now defines "normal variance" as the trailing 6-8 period range, so a single period-over-period swing no longer triggers a change.

  • ads (2.7.1 → 2.7.2): the operating check pulls the trailing 6-8 periods, calls HOLD when a metric sits inside that range, and treats three or more periods moving the same way as a real movement. Spend, tracking, and broken-page problems, and target-based stop thresholds such as cost per qualified lead above 1.5× TCPL, skip the range gate.
  • marketing-loops (1.8.1 → 1.8.2): the loop catalog applies the same trailing-range baseline to its performance loops.

  • conversion-tracking (1.0.2 → 1.0.3): two detector fixes. generic-ajax-form.js no longer skips a form whose own handler called preventDefault(), which every AJAX form does to send its request with fetch or XHR, so an inline success message after the submit now pushes form_submitted (#912). thank-you-page.js no longer counts a refresh or a back/forward return to the confirmation page as another conversion when COUNT_EVERY_VIEW is false; it reads the browser's navigation type, so a new arrival on the path still counts and single-page-app route changes keep the in-memory guard (#913). v2.14.3v2.14.3 — Shorter skill descriptions, so Claude Code shows all of them. Stable2026-11-09T19:09:12Z

Shorter skill descriptions, so Claude Code shows all of them.

  • Every skill (patch bump each): descriptions rewritten to 491 characters or fewer, 19,259 in total (down from 42,105). Claude Code caps the skill listing it sends the model at about 30,000 characters, and past the cap a skill appears as a bare name: before this release, 26 of 53 skills had no description in the listing even with the pack installed alone; now none do. Each description says what the skill does, the distinct situations it covers, its operative rule, and the neighbouring skill to use instead, starting with "When the user wants to". On the new trigger eval (Sonnet), the right skill was picked for 98.7% of prompts (from 96.2%) with the pack alone, 96.9% (from 94.3%) with 90 other skills installed, and 89.6% (from 81.1%) on held-out prompts written without reading the descriptions, with other skills installed.
  • Trigger eval (scripts/trigger-eval.mjs, evals/triggers/): runs prompts through headless Claude Code with the pack as a plugin and scores the first skill invoked. Includes 171 prompts across all skills, a 114-prompt held-out set, an option to install other skills alongside, and contract tests.
  • Description budget: scripts/check-versions.mjs now fails a PR if a description passes 500 characters or the pack passes 24,000. AGENTS.md and CONTRIBUTING.md explain the limit and how to test description changes.

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.105graphify 0.9.105 Stable2026-11-09T17:04:20Z

graphify 0.9.105

  • Fix: watch ignored directories (node_modules, .git, build output) are pruned from the Linux watch tree, so a large project no longer registers tens of thousands of wasted inotify watches and risks ENOSPC; the non-Linux recursive watcher is unchanged and still fires on every real change (#5074, #5091, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).

  • Fix: cpp a member-call chain routed through class fields declared in other headers (client.actions->Execute(), with value ., raw-pointer -> and single-arg unique_ptr/shared_ptr receivers) resolves to the field type's member across the include set, gated to a unique type and member and fail-closed otherwise; a typed local whose class is in another repo is parked for the cross-repo merge (#5098, thanks @xRedLeader).

  • Fix: markdown a code span naming an uppercase \-namespace class (\Foo\Bar, App\API) or a type-qualified method call (Type::member()) is kept as a symbol mention instead of being dropped, while a non-call Type::CONST/Type::class stays dropped (#5093, thanks @Adityakk9031).

  • Fix: ts/js a call through a destructured CommonJS barrel require (const { Thing } = require('./barrel') where the barrel re-exports Thing) resolves to the real declaration by following the barrel's exports, instead of dropping the edge (#5034, #5104, thanks @qq2514568380).

  • Fix: wiki the orphan sweep folds slugs with casefold (not lower), so two article names that collide only under full Unicode case-folding (Straße/STRASSE, Σ/ς) each keep their own file instead of one being deleted as an orphan; a file written this run is never swept (#5087, #5102, thanks @nothariharan).

  • Fix: build hyperedges are kept whole across an incremental graphify update: they are read from the nested graph slot a JSON-only writer uses, and after a deleted-source prune a hyperedge drops only its removed members (and itself only when none remain), instead of silently losing or leaving dangling hyperedges (#5081, #5103, thanks @nothariharan).

  • Fix: cache a degraded (under-producing) semantic cache entry no longer pins the corpus: after the shrink guard sees a re-extraction come back smaller than the stored graph, the entry is stamped partial so the next run re-dispatches it instead of replaying the degraded result forever (#5083, #5101, thanks @nothariharan).

  • Fix: affected graphify affected <file> seeds the file's nested member nodes (a method inside a class) transitively, not just one hop, so impact analysis no longer under-reports callers that bind to a nested member (#5094, thanks @Adityakk9031).

  • Fix: tree build_tree derives the common directory root from file parents, so a single-file or single-directory tree is no longer self-nested under the file itself (#5092, thanks @Adityakk9031).

  • Fix: query a label whose name is behind a schema or namespace qualifier (public.sweep_widget_state(), fmt.Println) is matched on its tail by the whole query and ranked above the per-token tiers, so it is no longer out-competed by single-token collision nodes and dropped from the seeds (#5080, #5095, thanks @sunhouy).

  • Fix: detect an incremental staleness check hashes a file whose stamp lands within the clock-skew window on either side of its mtime, so a same-tick rewrite that does not move mtime is still caught instead of being served stale (#5090, thanks @sunhouy).

  • Fix: path graphify path A B refuses an endpoint whose words appear nowhere in the graph (exit not-found) instead of returning a misleading route built from loosely-overlapping tokens (#5085, thanks @sunhouy).

  • Fix: csharp a classic extension-method call (x.Foo() where Foo is declared static ... Foo(this X x, ...)) binds to the extension method's declaration through an additive pass that runs after nominal dispatch, gated to a visible namespace, a matching this type and a unique applicable method; an instance method of the same name wins, a non-extension static or an unimported extension gets no edge, and ambiguity fails closed (#4828, #4849, thanks @Deepjyoti-Sarmah).

  • Fix: markdown a bare, unqualified free-function mention in prose (token, no () and no namespace) binds to a same-named function only when its defining file (or another symbol from it) is cited within the heading's section chain, instead of matching any same-named callable in the corpus; reference-style links ([text][label]) resolve at the use site (#4804, #4806, thanks @madanlalit).

  • Fix: export the to_json shrink guard no longer counts semantic sources that were replaced on re-extract or pruned as deletions as "lost", so a legitimate graphify update is not refused; a genuine unexplained shrink is still refused (#5107, thanks @khkar).

  • Fix: build a node whose file_type or confidence arrives as a structured value (list/dict) from a drifting LLM pass no longer crashes the validate/build membership checks: validation surfaces it as a schema error, and the build folds it to concept/INFERRED without fabricating a score (#5088, thanks @sdivyanshu90).

  • Fix: llm a splittable document below the character cap whose model output truncates is promoted to a full-range slice and bisected (and retried) instead of being kept as a truncated partial result; a non-splittable or code/binary unit still fails closed (#5072, #5078, thanks @aadilrizwan).

  • Feat: llm a custom OpenAI-compatible provider's extra_headers are forwarded to the client as default_headers, scoped to that provider and with any Authorization header dropped so it cannot override the configured credential (#3680, #4783, thanks @jaimemartinez).

  • Perf: extract whole-graph intermediates (the parse memo on the one-shot CLI, build/cluster/export working lists) are freed as soon as they are consumed, cutting peak memory on a large corpus; graph output is byte-identical (#5063, thanks @TPAteeq).

  • Fix: skillgen the claw/kiro/pi subagent prompt now tells the worker to write its chunk to CHUNK_PATH, so a disk-dispatch chunk is no longer returned inline and lost at the merge step (#5040, thanks @connhodendatabase).

  • Fix: deps the pypdf floor is raised to 6.21.4 in the pdf and all extras to pick up its security fixes (#5015, thanks @yotamleo).

The AST cache schema is bumped to 42; warm caches re-run the affected C++, Markdown and C# extractions.
